# Naming & Folder Conventions — GitClaudeUnrealTest

Owned by the **manager** agent. All agents MUST follow these. If a needed pattern is missing, the manager adds it here BEFORE issuing the task.

## Asset prefixes (Content Browser)

| Prefix | Type | Folder |
|--------|------|--------|
| BP_    | Blueprint class | Content/Blueprints/ |
| WBP_   | UMG widget | Content/UI/ |
| SM_    | Static mesh | Content/Meshes/ |
| SK_    | Skeletal mesh | Content/Characters/ |
| M_     | Material | Content/Materials/ |
| MI_    | Material instance | Content/Materials/Instances/ |
| T_     | Texture | Content/Textures/ |
| NS_    | Niagara system | Content/VFX/ |
| S_     | Sound | Content/Audio/ |
| DT_    | Data table | Content/Data/ |
| DA_    | Data asset (UDataAsset instance) | Content/Data/ |
| L_     | Level | Content/Maps/ |
| LS_    | Level Sequence (Sequencer) | Content/Cinematics/ |
| ABP_   | Anim Blueprint | Content/Characters/ |
| A_     | Anim Sequence | Content/Characters/Anims/ |
| AM_    | Anim Montage | Content/Characters/Anims/ |
| SKEL_  | Skeleton (shared rig) | Content/Characters/ |
| IK_    | IK Rig (retargeting) | Content/Characters/ |
| RTG_   | IK Retargeter | Content/Characters/ |
| IA_    | Input action | Content/Input/Actions/ |
| IMC_   | Input mapping context | Content/Input/ |

## Texture suffixes
`T_<Name>_D` base color · `T_<Name>_N` normal · `T_<Name>_R` roughness · `T_<Name>_M` metallic · `T_<Name>_E` emissive · `T_<Name>_ORM` packed Occlusion/Roughness/Metallic (LINEAR — sRGB off; added 2026-07-07, TRELLIS pipeline)

## C++ (Source/GitClaudeUnrealTest/)
- Classes: `A` actors, `U` UObjects/components, `F` structs, `E` enums, `I` interfaces
- One class per header/cpp pair; file name = class name without prefix
- Exception: pure data types (enums, structs, UInterfaces) may share a header-only file when they form one concept (e.g., `TeamId.h`)
- Gameplay-relevant members exposed with UPROPERTY/UFUNCTION; use TObjectPtr for UObject members
- **No shadowing inherited reflected members (coding law):** no local variable, function parameter, or loop variable may shadow an inherited reflected UPROPERTY — e.g. `Owner`/`PlayerState`/`Instigator`/`Controller` on AActor/APawn/AController, `Slot` on UWidget. UHT + the UE toolchain compile this as a HARD ERROR (C4457 param / C4458 member / C4459 global), not a warning. Rename the local (e.g. `OwnerState`, `IterPlayerState`, `SlotIndex`). **QA MUST scan every code task for this class of shadow pre-compile** — it slipped past QA twice in the M2 batch (TASK-025, TASK-029) and cost 2 build loops.
- **⚠️ MOST-VEXING-PARSE law (coding law, added 2026-08-02 — TASK-416, `SiegeAssistantSnapshot.cpp:337`).** A local declared with **parentheses** whose argument is `TypeName(identifier)` is parsed by C++ as a **FUNCTION DECLARATION**, not a variable — redundant parentheses around a parameter *name* are legal, so the compiler sees a parameter of type `TypeName` named `identifier`:
  ```cpp
  const TSoftObjectPtr<UDataTable> CardTableAsset(FSoftObjectPath(CardTablePath));   // ✗ declares a FUNCTION
  const TSoftObjectPtr<UDataTable> CardTableAsset{ FSoftObjectPath(CardTablePath) }; // ✓ braces CANNOT be a parameter list
  ```
  **Fix = brace-initialise.** The failure is two diagnostics on the FOLLOWING line, which is what makes it hard to read: applying `.` to a function name gives **`C2228`**, and the `const` local left without an initialiser gives **`C2737`**.
  - **⚠️ WHY IT HAD NEVER BITTEN BEFORE, AND WHY IT WILL RECUR: every shipped `TSoftObjectPtr` resolve in this repo dodges it by passing a STRING LITERAL, and a literal can never be a parameter name.** **Hoisting the path into a named constant — good practice, done so a log line and the resolve share one string — is exactly what re-opens the trap.** In a codebase full of soft-object resolves this will happen again to someone doing the right thing.
  - **🔍 THE DISTINGUISHING TEST (record it, it is how you tell these apart in seconds):** a genuinely missing complete type produces **`C2027` "use of undefined type"**. **`C2228` on a name that otherwise resolves fine is a PARSE problem, not an include problem** — adding a header will not fix it and the error will survive to the next gate.
- **Complete-type include law (coding law, added 2026-07-09):** any `.cpp` that dereferences OR upcasts a value returned as a forward-declared pointer must `#include` that type's full header. The classic trap is component accessors: `GetCapsuleComponent()`, `GetMesh()`, `GetCharacterMovement()`, `GetWorldTimerManager()`, etc. return pointers whose types are only forward-declared by the base header, so calling a method or `Cast<>`-ing the result needs the complete type in the translation unit (e.g. `#include "Components/CapsuleComponent.h"`). This compiles as a HARD ERROR (C2027 "use of undefined type" / C2227), not a warning. **TASK-110 (HeroCharacter.cpp) failed compile on exactly this** — an upcast of `GetCapsuleComponent()` with no `CapsuleComponent.h` include — and QA cannot see include-completeness from source review alone. **QA MUST scan every code task for a member/`Cast<>`/method call on a type whose header is not `#include`d in that .cpp** (forward declaration is not enough); flag it as a pre-compile finding.

## C++ layout — Siegebound gameplay
- All new Siegebound gameplay code lives in `Source/GitClaudeUnrealTest/Siegebound/`
- Framework classes carry the `Siege` name: `ASiegeGameMode`, `ASiegePlayerState`, `ASiegePlayerController`
- Gameplay actors use plain descriptive names: `ACastle`, `AHeroCharacter`, `ASummonedUnit`
- Never modify template code (`GitClaudeUnrealTest*` classes, `Variant_*` folders, `ThirdPerson/FloatingCastle.*`); subclassing template classes is allowed

## Blueprint subclasses of C++ classes
- Name = `BP_` + C++ class name without its prefix: `BP_HeroCharacter` (from `AHeroCharacter`) — in Content/Blueprints/
- Per-card unit blueprints: `BP_Unit_<CardID>` in Content/Blueprints/Units/ (e.g., `BP_Unit_Footman`)
- Per-card building blueprints: `BP_Building_<CardID>` in Content/Blueprints/Buildings/ (e.g., `BP_Building_ArrowTower`, `BP_Building_Wall`)
- Code spawns card actors by composed soft-class path from the CardID: `/Game/Blueprints/Units/BP_Unit_<CardID>.BP_Unit_<CardID>_C` (units/economy) and `/Game/Blueprints/Buildings/BP_Building_<CardID>.BP_Building_<CardID>_C` (buildings) — always null-safe (missing BP = refused play + log, never a crash)

## Per-card visual assets
- A card actor's visual mesh asset is `SM_<CardID>` in Content/Meshes/ (e.g., `SM_Footman`, `SM_ArrowTower`). This is a code contract: placement-ghost previews resolve `/Game/Meshes/SM_<CardID>` by string.
- The mesh component on card actors (ASummonedUnit, ABuilding) is named exactly `VisualMesh`.

## Card artwork (hand UI)
Added 2026-07-07 (TASK-077..081, Jonathan feature request: real card art on the hand-UI card faces). Every CardID in the roster gets exactly ONE illustration texture.
- **Texture asset:** `T_CardArt_<CardID>` in Content/UI/CardArt/ (`/Game/UI/CardArt/T_CardArt_<CardID>`), one per CardID, exactly **512×512** (square power-of-two — plenty for a hand-slot face). Deliberate exception to the prefix table's `T_` → Content/Textures/ row: card art is UI-owned and lives with the UI. Import settings: Texture Group = UI, sRGB on, default compression.
- **Source render:** PNG at `Content/RawAssets/CardArt/<CardID>.png`, checked into Git alongside the imported .uasset (mirrors the FBX raw-asset rule). `<CardID>` casing character-for-character from cards.csv row names.
- **Data path (§3.0 law):** the art reference is DT_Cards data, never hardcoded — FCardRow column `CardArt` (`TSoftObjectPtr<UTexture2D>`); CSV cell = the FULL object path `/Game/UI/CardArt/T_CardArt_<CardID>.T_CardArt_<CardID>`. Unset/unresolvable path ⇒ graceful text-only card face (today's presentation), log once, never a crash. Widgets never hardcode a CardID→texture mapping.
- **Resolution seam:** `UCardHandWidget` exposes null-safe `UFUNCTION(BlueprintCallable)` resolver(s) — `GetCardArtTexture(CardID)` + a preview-art equivalent (TASK-079). The three hand BIEs (`OnHandSlotUpdated` / `OnNextCardUpdated` / `OnCardRefusedMessage`) keep their signatures: BIE PARAMS stay float/int/bool/byte/FString only; UObject returns are allowed on BlueprintCallable functions, which is why the resolver pattern is the law here.
- **Face composition:** art is the BACKGROUND layer of the card face; DisplayName + cost text overlay on top and must stay legible (translucent contrast strip / shadow behind text is allowed). The artwork itself contains NO baked-in text. Art images are HitTestInvisible — clicks belong to the play/discard buttons (M1 WARN-4 posture). Style may be blockout-tier stylized (premium art = M7) but each card must read at ~150 px: one dominant subject, strong silhouette, distinct per-card color key, team-agnostic palette (cards are player-neutral).
- **🎨 "DISTINCT KEY COLOUR" IS A MEASURED BAR, NOT AN EYEBALL — and the wheel is more crowded than anyone assumed (added 2026-08-01, TASK-373).** The roster's own nearest-neighbour behaviour is the yardstick, computed as **CIEDE2000 (ΔE2000) from each shipped key to *its own* nearest neighbour**: **n = 29 · min 4.07 (Ogre↔PlateArmor) · p10 4.13 · median 7.85 · max 12.53 (Pickpocket↔Archer)**. **Only 5 of 29 shipped cards clear ΔE 10** — so an arbitrary "ΔE ≥ 10" bar would fail most of the existing roster and is the wrong test. **LAW: a new key is judged against the roster's own distribution** (report the ΔE2000 to the nearest shipped key and its percentile vs the median 7.85), **never against a number invented for the task, and never by eye.** Choose by grid search over the intended hue band maximising min-ΔE2000 to all shipped keys.
- **🖼️ CARD-FACE FIDELITY TIER — the M7-tier standard, and the tool that produces it (Jonathan ruling 2026-08-01, TASK-373).** The 29 shipped card faces are primitive "board-game token" renders; the Sorcerer's face renders the **real `SM_<CardID>` game mesh** and is visibly higher fidelity. **Jonathan ruled the higher-fidelity face is the new standard and the other 29 are re-rendered to match** (~4 s/card headless now that the path exists) — the token renders are legacy, not a style choice, and no new card may be authored as a token.
  - **Tool (NEW, named here before the task is issued):** `Tools/ArtPipeline/cardart_render.py` — the durable home for the card-face render. `Tools/**/*.py` is **CODE ⇒ the tooling QA gate applies before commit** (same law as `concept_generate.py` / `meshy_generate.py` / `rig_character.py`), and it mirrors their CLI + exit-code discipline. It is the ONE sanctioned producer of `Content/RawAssets/CardArt/<CardID>.png`.
  - **Lane isolation still binds in BOTH directions:** `cardart_render.py` belongs to the **card-art chain** and writes ONLY `Content/RawAssets/CardArt/` (+ its own gitignored cache) — it never writes `/Game/Meshes/`, `/Game/Textures/`, `Cache/<CardID>/` or `pipeline_manifest.json`; and the TRELLIS.2/Meshy pipeline still never writes card art. It **reads** the shipped meshes/materials as read-only donors.
  - **⚠️ PER-ASSET RENDER-CAMERA FACING IS NOT A CONSTANT — READ IT, DO NOT ASSUME. (Rule STRENGTHENED and its EXAMPLE CORRECTED 2026-08-02, TASK-375-FACING-FIX.)** ⚠️ **The example as first written is now STALE and INVERTED: `Sorcerer.fbx` fronted `+Y` only BEFORE the source fix; as shipped today it fronts the pipeline-normal `−Y`, and the card's baked camera went `MODEL_YAW_DEG` 196 → 16** (the 180° compensation removed, only the ¾ kick remains). **The RULE is unchanged and now better founded:** exported facing is **generation-scoped** — it flipped for the whole unit lane on 2026-07-28 (`_ue_handedness_precomp`; see "Fleet Meshy remaster" → "THE UNIT LANE HAS NOW MET THE PRE-COMP") — so **any batch renderer DERIVES or VERIFIES per-asset facing and RECORDS it, and never hardcodes one camera azimuth for the fleet.** Verify with the `−Y`-camera + `Footman.fbx`-control render, by positive identity cues.
  - **🎥 THE CARD RENDER IS CYCLES, NOT EEVEE (CORRECTION, 2026-08-02 — supersedes the EEVEE record in `handoffs/TASK-373-artist.md` PART 1 §2 and the TASK-077/TASK-303 recipe FOR THIS TIER).** Measured in an isolated probe on this machine: headless Blender 5.1 **EEVEE renders the identical textured scene FLAT — channel spread 0.060 vs Cycles' 0.281.** Cycles is also what `refine_trellis_glb.py` already uses for its beauty preview. **The 29 legacy token faces WERE EEVEE and that history is correct; every M7-tier face — the Sorcerer's and every re-render — is CYCLES.** Everything else in the studio recipe (65 mm / 36 mm sensor, Standard/None view transform, 512×512, the halo, the light roles) is unchanged.
  - **🪤 TWO RENDER-SCRIPT TRAPS, both measured while rebuilding the Sorcerer face (2026-08-02) — they will bite any future card/preview script:** (1) **`obj.data.materials.clear()` RESETS every polygon's `material_index` to 0** (measured `{1: 14630, 0: 370}` → `{0: 15000}`), silently putting the whole figure on slot 0 and rendering it flat neutral grey — **assign slots IN PLACE (`materials[0] = …`, `materials[1] = …`)**; (2) **Principled's default 0.5 specular puts a NEUTRAL white sheen on the backdrop**, pinning the corner key's red channel no matter how far an albedo solve pushes it — **make the backdrop fully matte.**
  - Everything else in this section is unchanged and still binds every re-render: **512×512 exactly**, Texture Group UI, sRGB on, no baked-in text, HitTestInvisible, team-agnostic palette, one dominant subject, reads at ~150 px, and the measured key-colour bar above. A re-render is a **same-path overwrite** of `Content/RawAssets/CardArt/<CardID>.png` → `/Game/UI/CardArt/T_CardArt_<CardID>` — **never delete+recreate** (the CSV `CardArt` cell is a full object path and would break).
- **⚠️ THE GREEN-TEAL ARC IS THE SECOND-MOST CROWDED REGION ON THE WHEEL — the plan's "jade is unused" note was WRONG** (it checked only the violet family). Measured against all 29 shipped keys, the real neighbours in the jade band are **Pickpocket 124.1° · Archer 134.8° · Cleric 178.2° · BallistaTower 188.1° · CrystalTower 190.1° · Footman 198.0°**, so the binding constraint is **Archer/Pickpocket**, not the violets. Shipped Sorcerer key = grid-search argmax **hue 168.0 / sat 0.633 / val 0.310, rgb8 (29,79,69)**, nearest shipped ΔE2000 **10.14** (Archer) ⇒ ~83rd percentile. **Do not assume any hue band is free — measure it.**

## Data-driven card stats (GDD §3.0)
- Source of truth: `Docs/Data/cards.csv` (checked into Git), imported as `/Game/Data/DT_Cards` with row struct `FCardRow`
- Row name = CardID in PascalCase, no spaces (e.g., `Footman`, `ArrowTower`); code and blueprints reference cards by CardID FName; `DisplayName` carries the spaced human name ("Arrow Tower")
- Never hardcode a stat that exists in the table
- FCardRow columns beyond the GDD §4 stat columns (registry — CSV header must match UPROPERTY names 1:1):
  - `DeckCount` (int32) — copies of this card in the default 50-card deck (GDD §3.4); all DeckCount values must sum to exactly 50; 0 = not in the default deck. **M4 note:** the M4 test deck (TASK-053) repurposes DeckCount as an expanded 22-card 50-count deck so Set II is reachable until the M6 deck-builder — still sums to 50, each ≤ MaxCopies.
  - `bRanged` (bool) — true if the card's attack is delivered by a homing projectile (GDD §3.0) instead of melee contact
  - `CardArt` (TSoftObjectPtr<UTexture2D>) — hand-UI card illustration; CSV cell = full object path `/Game/UI/CardArt/T_CardArt_<CardID>.T_CardArt_<CardID>`; unset = text-only face fallback (added 2026-07-07, TASK-079; law in "Card artwork (hand UI)")
  - **M4 keyword/behavior columns (Set II, TASK-053):** typed one-per-concept, sparse (defaults shown):
    - `bCharge` (bool, false) — Charge keyword: first attack after ≥2 s uninterrupted movement deals 2× (GDD §3.0). (Cavalry)
    - `bSlayer` (bool, false) — Slayer keyword: 2× damage vs targets with MaxHP ≥ 150 (GDD §3.0). (Pikeman)
    - `bSuicide` (bool, false) — unit explodes on contact/death then dies, dealing its Damage as AoE over `AoERadius` (GDD §4). (Sapper)
    - `SwarmCount` (int32, 0) — Swarm keyword: >0 ⇒ playing the card spawns this many copies in a 300-unit circle for one cost (GDD §3.0). (Militia Mob = 4)
    - `AoERadius` (float, 0) — splash radius for area attackers; 0 = single target. (Sapper 250, Bomb Tower 250)
    - `MinRange` (float, 0) — inner blind-spot radius; the actor cannot fire at targets closer than this (GDD §4). (Ballista Tower 300)
    - `SpawnCardID` (FName, None) / `SpawnInterval` (float, 0) / `Lifetime` (float, 0) — spawner building: spawns `SpawnCardID` every `SpawnInterval` s, self-destructs after `Lifetime` s (GDD §4). (Barracks = Footman / 8 / 60)
  - The keyword-set token approach is deferred. **M5 columns (TASK-097): registry continues in "Spells & Set III (M5)"** — `SpellEffect` (ESpellEffect), `EffectDuration`, `MaxTargets`, `GoldSteal`, `ChainTargets`, `ChainFalloff`; spells reuse `Damage`/`AoERadius`.
- Mechanic RULES (not per-card stats) — e.g., active miner cap 6, building clearance 200, overtime at 420 s, Swarm 300-unit spawn circle, Charge 2 s / 2× multipliers, Slayer 150-HP / 2× threshold, Deep Mine +2 gold/s, Masons 300 HP over 10 s, hero-upgrade magnitudes (§3.10), Rally 600/25%/5 s/20 s — are UPROPERTY defaults in the owning class with a `// GDD §x.x` comment; they do not get CSV columns

## Team contract
- Enum `ETeamId { Blue, Red }`. The local player is always Blue; the enemy is Red
- Team master material `/Game/Materials/M_TeamColor` exposes a vector parameter named exactly `TeamColor`
- Instances: `/Game/Materials/Instances/MI_TeamColor_Blue` = linear (0.05, 0.30, 1.00); `/Game/Materials/Instances/MI_TeamColor_Red` = linear (1.00, 0.10, 0.05)
- **Team-driven visuals (M3):** team-owned actors (units, buildings, miners) apply the matching `MI_TeamColor_<Team>` to their `VisualMesh` slot 0 in `BeginPlay` from their `Team` value; the Blue material authored on the BP is only the design-time placeholder, so the bot's Red units recolor at spawn. Gold nodes are the exception — they carry the authored `M_GoldGlow` emissive regardless of team.

## World axes (arena contract)
**BATTLEFIELD SCALE — 4× WIDENED (M6.5, 2026-07-10, Jonathan directive "make the distance that they [the castles] are apart 4 times larger").** Castle separation went from 4000 (±2000) to **16000 (±8000)**. The centerline stays X=0 and ALL placement-half / mirror / spawn rules are unchanged — they key off the centerline, not the castle distance. Gold nodes stay **800 units in front of each castle**, so they moved WITH the castles (∓1200 → ±7200) to preserve the miner economy geometry — NOT left at ±1200 (which would strand miners ~6800 units from their node). KillZ (−2000) is VERTICAL fall-death — UNCHANGED (it is not a horizontal-spacing value). Pre-M6.5 values are recorded in-line for history.
- In `L_Arena`: Blue castle at (X=−8000, Y=0), Red castle at (X=+8000, Y=0); the centerline is the plane X=0 (castles were ±2000 pre-M6.5)
- Blue placement half: X <= 0; Red half: X >= 0 (unchanged — centerline-relative)
- Level marker actors are TargetPoints named `<Purpose>Anchor_<Team>`: `CastleAnchor_Blue` (−8000,0), `CastleAnchor_Red` (+8000,0)
- Gold nodes (GDD §5, 800 units in front of each castle): `AGoldNode` instances `GoldNode_Blue` at (−7200, 0), `GoldNode_Red` at (+7200, 0) (were ∓1200 pre-M6.5)
- Team-owned level instances are named `<Thing>_<Team>` (e.g., `Castle_Blue`, `GoldNode_Red`)
- **Code fallbacks that MUST match these (TASK-133):** `ASiegeBotController::CastleRedFallbackLocation = (8000,0,0)` and `GoldNodeRedFallbackLocation = (7200,0,0)`. There is NO Blue fallback constant (the player side uses live `TActorIterator<ACastle>` / `TActorIterator<AGoldNode>` lookups). `BotCenterlineSpawnX = 350` is CENTERLINE-relative and STAYS 350 (the centerline did not move; a playtest may revisit whether the bot over-commits units across the wider field). PlayerStart moves outward to ≈(−6800, 0, 100) — a LEVEL actor (the SiegeGameMode comment is updated to match).
- **PACING NOTE (flagged to Jonathan):** cross-map unit march time is now ~4× longer; the miner→own-node economy is UNCHANGED (nodes moved with the castles).

## Arena terrain & environment (M4.5) — SUPERSEDED (placement approach) by "Battlefield & procedural terrain (M6.5)" below
**SUPERSEDED 2026-07-10 by M6.5.** Jonathan's 2026-07-10 battlefield directive changed the terrain approach from hand-placed **mirror-symmetric** hills+obstacles to **RUNTIME PROCEDURAL RANDOMIZED** scatter (a new layout each match) + a 4× wider arena. RETIRED (never integrated — M4.5 was parked awaiting the Fab drop): the mirror-symmetry law, the flat-pad law, the hand-placed `Hill_/Tree_/Rock_<NN>_<Side>` mirror-pair instances, and the `SM_ArenaTerrain` / `SM_Hill_01` / `SM_Tree_01/02` / `SM_Rock_01` CONFORM targets (M6.5 soft-references Fab meshes IN PLACE for variety instead of conforming to fixed names). SURVIVES (still compiled + valid): (a) the `Terrain` / `Obstacle` actor-tag code contracts, (b) the placement-clearance code — `MaxPlacementSlopeDegrees`, `ObstaclePlacementClearance`, "Too steep" / "Too close to obstacles" (TASK-093, qa-passed), and (c) the projectile-destroy-on-`Terrain`/`Obstacle` law. **BUT NOTE (M6.5 blocking is achieved at the COLLISION/navmesh level, NOT via these tags):** M6.5's scattered obstacles are HISM INSTANCES, not tagged actors, so the tag-reading (b)/(c) code does NOT see them — units block/route via HISM collision + Dynamic navmesh (that is the hard requirement, and it works independently), but two MINOR GAPS are accepted for this milestone and flagged as possible follow-ups: a player could place a building overlapping a scattered obstacle (the `Obstacle`-tag clearance won't fire — keep-clear keeps obstacles out of the main build lane, mitigating it), and a projectile won't self-destruct on a scattered tree/rock (cosmetic pass-through). Neither is a hard failure; revisit only if playtest flags them. The rest of this section is HISTORICAL REFERENCE only; the live terrain law is M6.5 below.

Added 2026-07-08 (TASK-091..096, Jonathan milestone directive: gameplay terrain pass — grass, hills, trees INSIDE the playable arena; amends GDD §5 "mostly open battlefield"; binding rulings in TASKBOARD "M4.5 manager decisions"). **AMENDED same day (Jonathan Fab directive):** source assets come from Fab packs Jonathan drops into `Content/Fab/<Pack>/` (FAB-001..004 in `.claude/pipeline/fab/FAB-REQUESTS.md`; drop instructions `Content/Fab/README_DROP_ZONE.md`) — donors are READ-ONLY and get CONFORMED to the target names below (duplicate into /Game/, rename, budgets/collision/origin per this section; target names ARE the law regardless of donor names). **Rock is added scope** under the same law as trees. Environment assets are NEUTRAL — the two-slot TeamRegion contract does NOT apply (deliberate deviation from the textured-mesh conform rule).
- **Terrain:** target `SM_ArenaTerrain` in Content/Meshes/ (`/Game/Meshes/SM_ArenaTerrain`) — the unified arena floor: 6400×3200 footprint, origin at base-plane center with the flat walk surface at origin Z, solid ≥100-unit underside/skirt (no holes at the edges) — placed at (0,0,0) it reproduces the old ArenaGround slab's walk surface at Z=0, so every existing actor transform stays valid. **Fab-composite alternative (conform decision at TASK-091):** a flat base ground + walkable hill meshes `SM_Hill_01`(+ two-digit variants) placed as instances. Either way: **Use Complex Collision As Simple** on all walkable terrain (hulls cannot carry hills); Nanite OFF; ≤60k tris for the unified mesh; UV layer `UVMap`.
- **Level instances (terrain):** actor `ArenaTerrain` at origin; hill instances (composite path) `Hill_<NN>_<Side>` in exact mirror pairs. ALL walkable-terrain actors (ground AND hills) carry actor tag **`Terrain`** (exact string — projectile-collision contract).
- **Ground material:** `M_ArenaGround` (Content/Materials/), stylized grass per §6 (no flat single color — macro variation required; slope-darkened dirt on hill flanks allowed). Textures conformed from the Fab grass pack take `T_ArenaGrass_*` names (Content/Textures/) when duplicated into /Game/. Grass-blade foliage is M7-tier unless trivially within the §6 perf budget (TASK-091 flags the call).
- **Trees & rocks (obstacles):** targets `SM_Tree_01`, `SM_Tree_02`, `SM_Rock_01` (+ variants, two-digit law) in Content/Meshes/; donor materials conformed under `M_Tree` / `M_Rock` (Content/Materials/; MI hue variants in Content/Materials/Instances/ allowed). ≤4k tris each; Nanite OFF. Collision is **FOOTPRINT-ONLY** (tree trunk / rock base): ≤2 authored-or-kept simple hulls hugging the footprint; tree canopy has NO collision. The footprint blocks navmesh (carves), movement, building placement, and projectiles.
- **Obstacle level instances:** `Tree_<NN>_<Side>` / `Rock_<NN>_<Side>`, Side ∈ `Blue` (X<0) / `Red` (X>0); `<NN>` pairs mirror exactly: `Tree_01_Blue` at (−x, y) ⇔ `Tree_01_Red` at (+x, y), same mesh variant per pair. EVERY obstacle instance (tree AND rock) carries actor tag **`Obstacle`** (exact string — the placement-clearance + projectile contracts read `Obstacle`, so new obstacle types never require code changes).
- **Mirror-symmetry law (GDD §5 amendment):** the arena stays fair by geometry — the terrain heightfield mirrors exactly across the X=0 centerline plane (H(x,y) = H(−x,y)) and every obstacle placement has an exact mirror twin. The centerline/placement-halves rule and the gold-node positions (±1200, 0) remain functional and unchanged.
- **Flat-pad law (Z=0, near-zero slope):** castle pads radius 700 at (±2000,0); gold-node pads radius 400 at (±1200,0); main lane |Y| ≤ 300 between the castle pads (the §3.3 ~10 s miner walk stays flat); centerline strip |X| ≤ 300. Hills and trees never intrude into pads or flat lanes; hill crowns (r200) stay tree-free.
- **High ground is PHYSICAL ONLY:** elevation grants NO stat bonuses (nothing enters cards.csv). Hills: walkable faces ≤ 35° (navmesh default 44° with margin); each hill crowns in a near-flat top (≤10°, r200 — legally placeable). Max terrain height 250.
- **Placement on terrain (mechanic rules → UPROPERTY defaults with GDD § comments, NOT CSV):** `MaxPlacementSlopeDegrees` = 20 — buildings refused on steeper ground (HUD "Too steep"); `ObstaclePlacementClearance` = 150 — buildings refused within 150 units (2D) of any `Obstacle`-tagged actor (HUD "Too close to obstacles"); both pre-checked before gold moves (net-zero refusal law). Units/miners need only a navmesh-valid point (unchanged). The placement ghost projects to the terrain SURFACE height and stays upright (no normal tilt). Castle-roof refusal + 200 building-vs-building clearance unchanged.
- **Projectile terrain law:** homing projectiles are DESTROYED (zero damage) on impact with walkable terrain or obstacle footprints (tags `Terrain`/`Obstacle`) — and deliberately do NOT collide with walls/buildings/castles/units beyond shipped behavior (archer-behind-own-wall comp and §3.0 castle scaling preserved). Target acquisition stays range-only (no LOS checks) as of M4.5.

## Battlefield & procedural terrain (M6.5)
Added 2026-07-10 (TASK-133..137; Jonathan directive: *"start creating the terrain/battlefield the characters fight on … space out the castles … 4 times larger … a grassy terrain filled with rocks, trees, and hills … randomly generated at the start of each match … choose what you think will look best … use as many assets as possible (that still make sense) for variety."*). SUPERSEDES the parked M4.5 hand-placed approach (see the supersede note on that section above). Two parts: **PART 1** = 4× castle spacing (see "World axes (arena contract)" — a level move + a CODE fallback sync, no new assets); **PART 2** = a RUNTIME procedural scatter of trees/rocks/hills/grass, re-seeded each match.

**Fab source assets (READ-ONLY donors, template-donor rule):** Jonathan imported the packs; agents **SOFT-REFERENCE the donor meshes IN PLACE** (never conform/rename/duplicate to fixed `SM_` names — the point is MAX VARIETY cheaply). Known pack roots (art-director browses ALL of them and curates the best-looking, variety-maximizing set): trees `/Game/Tree_Pack_1/Meches/…` (`SM_Highpoly_Tree_1..12` + `SM-Mobile_Tree_1..12` — PREFER the MOBILE low-poly variants for scatter); grass/plants `/Game/Realistic_Grass_and_plant/`, `/Game/Megaplant_Library/`; rocks `/Game/Realistic_Rocks/`, `/Game/Fab/Rocks/highpoly_rocks_free_download/`; hills `/Game/Fab/Stone_Hills_FREE/stone_hill/StaticMeshes/stone_hill`. DO NOT scatter character packs (`Prickly_Knight`, `sA_StylizedWizardSet`), VFX packs, or castle-wall/siege packs (a few siege props from `MedievalCastleEnvironmentAndSiegeWeaponProps` MAY be dressing at the art-director's judgment — flag if used; it is not the core ask).

**Scatter actor:** `ASiegeBattlefieldScatter` (AActor subclass, `BattlefieldScatter.h/.cpp` in `Source/GitClaudeUnrealTest/Siegebound/`), ONE instance placed in `L_Arena` named `BattlefieldScatter`. It owns one **HISM (`UHierarchicalInstancedStaticMeshComponent`) per unique scattered mesh** — instancing is MANDATORY for perf (donor meshes are heavy). Fills the arena at match start via a seeded `FRandomStream`; exposes `GenerateScatter()` / `ClearScatter()`; null-safe (no config = no-op, log once). Log category `LogSiegeTerrain` (one grep-able line with the chosen seed so a layout is reproducible).

**Scatter config (data-driven — density/meshes are content, not code):** `USiegeScatterConfig` (`UDataAsset` subclass, `ScatterConfig.h/.cpp`); instance asset `DA_BattlefieldScatter` at `/Game/Data/DA_BattlefieldScatter`. Holds a per-layer `FScatterLayer` (roughly `{ TArray<TSoftObjectPtr<UStaticMesh>> Meshes; int32 InstanceCount/Density; FVector2D ScaleRange; bool bRandomYaw; bool bBlocking (default FALSE); region-bias; float MinSpacing; }`, programmer picks the exact shape + sane defaults) for the four layers **TREES / ROCKS / HILLS / GRASS**. The actor reads `TObjectPtr<USiegeScatterConfig> ScatterConfig` (EditDefaultsOnly); unset/empty ⇒ graceful no-op. `int32 OverrideSeed` (0 = random each match) + `bool bReRandomizeOnMatchReset` (default true — Play Again gets a fresh layout) live on the actor/config.

**Ground:** the EXISTING arena floor slab is SCALED (build-master) to cover the widened arena and gets a new grass material `M_BattlefieldGround` (Content/Materials/; §6 stylized, macro variation, NO flat single color; MI hue variants allowed in Content/Materials/Instances/). Grass BLADES are a scatter LAYER (HISM), **NOT a UE Landscape** — MCP has no Landscape create/sculpt route (M4.5 ruling 3; a real Landscape would need Jonathan's manual editor work).

**Collision / blocking — BLOCKING obstacles (Jonathan ruling 2026-07-10, decision #1 = the bigger path: rocks/trees/hills physically block unit movement AND carve the navmesh):** obstacle layers (TREES / ROCKS / HILLS) have instances that **block the Pawn channel** and set **`bCanEverAffectNavigation = true`** so they generate nav obstacles; the GRASS layer stays non-blocking decoration (`bBlocking=false`, NoCollision). Instancing choice — HISM-with-nav (preferred for perf; ISM/HISM instances DO support navigation collision) vs individual actors — is the programmer's call, keep the perf budget.
- **Runtime dynamic navmesh is MANDATORY:** `L_Arena`'s `RecastNavMesh` must be `RuntimeGeneration = Dynamic` (project Navigation setting / the nav-mesh actor). Without it, runtime-scattered obstacles do NOT affect pathing and units walk straight through them. Set + verified at TASK-136 (drop a test blocker → confirm the nav carves + a path reroutes).
- **TRAVERSABILITY GUARANTEE (NON-NEGOTIABLE):** the scatter must NEVER wall off a side, trap units, or block a spawn/castle. A match where units cannot reach the enemy castle is a HARD FAILURE. `ASiegeBattlefieldScatter` guarantees a navigable castle-to-castle path EVERY match via: a reserved clear central corridor + the keep-clear radii below, AND/OR a post-placement reachability validation (re-roll / cull offending instances until a Blue-castle → Red-castle path is confirmed).
- **Placement symmetry — ⚠️ SUPERSEDED 2026-08-01 by the 180°-ROTATIONAL LAW.** The LIVE law is the section **"Ancient Grounds + Sorcerer + 180° terrain symmetry (2026-08-01)"** below: ALL terrain (trees, rocks, hills, grass, slabs, mines, ancient grounds) is generated on the **BLUE half (X ≤ 0)** and duplicated by a **180° rotation about the map center** — `(X, Y) → (−X, −Y)`, `yaw' = yaw + 180`, scale UNCHANGED. **The retired wording is quoted here on purpose so no future agent "restores" it:** ~~"Placement symmetry — ASYMMETRIC organic random (Jonathan ruling, FLAGGED, default asymmetric)… placement is FULLY ASYMMETRIC random… Jonathan may switch to a mirror-symmetric mode if matches read as unfair at playtest (expose it as a config toggle — do NOT silently impose mirror-symmetry)."~~ That flagged 2026-07-10 decision was Jonathan's and **his 2026-08-01 directive replaces it verbatim**: *"I now want all terrain (trees, mines, hills, rocks, etc) to be '180 degree turn' mirrored instead of X-mirror. An easy method is to generate one half of the map, then duplicate it and turn it 180 degrees."* Asymmetric scatter is no longer the default and no longer the toggle default; **re-introducing it needs a NEW Jonathan ruling.** The X-mirror mode (`bMirrorSymmetric`) is retired in the same stroke — it was a fake reflection (translate + yaw+180, self-documented at `BattlefieldScatter.cpp:1219-1221`) and a true rotation supersedes it.

**Keep-clear zones (manager ruling — decision #3 = YES; now doubly important, it is part of the traversability guarantee):** trees/rocks/hills are excluded from the castle pads (r ~900 around ±8000), gold-node pads (r ~500 around ±7200), the PlayerStart/spawn area, and the central combat corridor (the main lane, |Y| ≤ ~400, that units march along) — so nothing blocks a castle, a spawn, the miner economy, or the lane. GRASS ignores keep-clear (lush everywhere, non-blocking). Radii are config values — Jonathan can make it denser/riskier at playtest.

**Perf (heightened by blocking):** blocking + highpoly + a Dynamic-navmesh rebuild on every scatter is costly. HISM everywhere; prefer the low-poly/mobile mesh variants or LODs; cap per-layer instance counts in the config. The §6 60 fps target is a human WATCH at Jonathan's playtest (no machine stat route — learnings); TASK-137 records best-effort FPS timings and flags if blocking counts must drop.

## Climbable terrain (M6.6)
Added 2026-07-14 (TASK-138..145; Jonathan directive: make the battlefield hills/rocks CLIMBABLE by the hero — root-cause investigation established this is BY-DESIGN from M6.5's route-around scatter; **UNPARKS the M4.5 "Gameplay terrain pass" intent**, TASK-091..096). **Root cause of the un-climbable hills:** the scatter applies UNIFORM scale (`BattlefieldScatter.cpp:247`, `FVector(Scale)`), so face angles are SCALE-INVARIANT — scaling the squashed `stone_hill` dome makes it taller AND wider, its flank still goes near-vertical at the rim (a ~4 m unclimbable skirt). No jump buff fixes a dome; the fix is purpose-built CONVEX hill meshes with ≤30° faces + flat crowns, under BOTH the character's 44.76° `WalkableFloorAngle` AND Recast's 44° `AgentMaxSlope`, so hero AND units climb with essentially no movement retune. Four LOCKED decisions (Jonathan, 2026-07-14): (1) M6.5 already committed by Jonathan @ `6a4c17d "battlefield created"` (PUSHED) — GATE 0 satisfied, no build-master M6.5 commit; (2) arena Y widened ±2400 → ±4000; (3) terrain BLOCKS projectiles (arrows die on rocks/hills/tree-trunks — accepted balance change); (4) units climb too (navmesh generates OVER hills — closes the melee-can't-reach-a-crowned-hero exploit). This section is the LIVE law; the M4.5 "Arena terrain & environment" section stays SUPERSEDED (history only).

- **Climbable hill meshes:** three purpose-built convex meshes `SM_Hill_01` / `SM_Hill_02` / `SM_Hill_03` (knoll / hill / ridge) at `/Game/Meshes/` (`/Game/Meshes/SM_Hill_0N`); raw FBX at `Content/RawAssets/Hill_0N.fbx` (raw-asset rule — the FBX is checked into Git alongside the imported .uasset). They REPLACE the unclimbable `stone_hill` dome in the Hill scatter layer.

  | Mesh | Base | Crown | Height | Target max face angle |
  |---|---|---|---|---|
  | `SM_Hill_01` knoll | r 700 | r 220 | 250 | ~27.5° |
  | `SM_Hill_02` hill  | r 1100 | r 320 | 400 | ~27° |
  | `SM_Hill_03` ridge | 2200×1700 | 1400×350 | 350 | ~27.5° |

- **Climbable-geometry law (THE gate):** every hill face angle ≤ **30°** (under BOTH the character's 44.76° `WalkableFloorAngle` AND Recast's 44° `AgentMaxSlope` → hero AND units climb, essentially no movement retune); crown near-flat ≤ **8°**; a toe fillet ≥ **120 cm** where the flank meets the ground (NO near-vertical skirt — the `stone_hill` defect); **CONVEX geometry, NO undercuts** (a single hull can't represent concavity — a "ridge" is one stretched dome, never a saddle); ≤ **1200 tris**; **Nanite OFF**; origin at base-center (z_min = 0); UV layer `UVMap`. Collision authored via `generate_convex_collisions(hull_count=1)` — exactly ONE convex hull matching the render mesh (a multi-hull or box hull would re-introduce an unclimbable step). **Acceptance = READBACK, not vibe** (TASK-088/135 technique): report `convexElems == 1`, `hull ZMax == mesh ZMax` (proves the crown is not bulged), and the MEASURED max face-normal-vs-+Z angle (that number is the gate, ≤30°). Material: assign the EXISTING `/Game/Materials/Instances/MI_BattlefieldGround` to slot 0 (world-XY projection → a 27° flank stretches only ~1.12×, so the hill reads continuous with the ground); **NO new material**.

- **Hero terrain-movement tuning (`AHeroCharacter` ONLY):** three tunables on `AHeroCharacter` (`HeroCharacter.h/.cpp`), NEVER on the template base `GitClaudeUnrealTestCharacter` (template law, §"C++ layout"), each `UPROPERTY(EditAnywhere, BlueprintReadOnly, Category="Siegebound|Movement")`, applied via a helper `ApplyTerrainMovementTuning()` called from BOTH the constructor AND `BeginPlay` (mirrors the existing `ApplyMovementSpeed()` at `HeroCharacter.cpp:45,82`):

  | Property | Current | Target |
  |---|---|---|
  | `HeroMaxStepHeight` | 45 | **50** |
  | `HeroWalkableFloorAngle` | 44.76 | **50** |
  | `HeroJumpZVelocity` | 500 | **600** |

  The `Hero` prefix on all three is MANDATORY and load-bearing: it avoids SHADOWING the identically-named `UCharacterMovementComponent` fields (`MaxStepHeight` / `WalkableFloorAngle` / `JumpZVelocity`) these values drive — an un-prefixed name collides as the **C4457 / C4458 / C4459** shadow HARD ERROR (the CONVENTIONS shadow law, §"C++"), not a warning. The retune is comfort/margin ONLY; the ≤30° faces are already climbable without it (`GravityScale` / `AirControl` unchanged).

- **`"Terrain"` actor tag on `ASiegeBattlefieldScatter`:** the scatter actor's constructor adds `Tags.Add(FName(TEXT("Terrain")))` — exact string `Terrain` (NOT `Obstacle`). `AProjectile::FindEnvironmentImpact` already object-traces the scatter HISMs and filters by owner-actor tag, so this SINGLE tag closes the projectile pass-through (decision #3: arrows die on rocks/hills/tree-trunks). Regression to hold: a tower on a crown must still shoot units below without its arrows dying on its OWN hill (PIE checklist #7; fallback = drop the tag).

- **Scatter collision-channel law (real geometry vs. proxy):**
  - **Real-geometry blockers (rocks / slabs / hills)** — the HISM blocks `ECC_Pawn` + `ECC_Visibility` + `ECC_Camera` and sets `bFillCollisionUnderneathForNavmesh = true` (Visibility+Camera close projectile pass-through and stop the camera clipping inside a mound when the hero stands on a crown; the nav-underneath fill lets units climb OVER the hill).
  - **Leave `ECC_WorldStatic` on Ignore** — `GroundZAt` (`BattlefieldScatter.cpp:459`) traces WorldStatic for the placement-ghost ground height; blocking it would break placement.

- **Tree collision-proxy contract (invisible-cylinder proxy per visual tree):** a tree's VISUAL HISM carries NO collision — `NoCollision` + `bCanEverAffectNavigation = false`. Blocking is delegated to a PAIRED proxy HISM (exactly one proxy HISM per visual mesh): `SetVisibility(false)` + `SetCastShadow(false)`, `QueryOnly` collision that blocks **Pawn ONLY** (NOT Visibility/Camera — else the placement ghost snaps to the invisible cylinder), `bCanEverAffectNavigation = true` + `bFillCollisionUnderneathForNavmesh = true`. `FScatterLayer` gains `TSoftObjectPtr<UStaticMesh> CollisionProxyMesh`, `FVector CollisionProxyScale = (1,1,1)`, `float CollisionProxyZOffset = 0`; a layer with `CollisionProxyMesh` set uses the proxy system (used for trees: visual canopy + trunk render, an engine `Cylinder` proxy blocks the trunk footprint). The actor keeps a `TMap<UHISM*, UHISM*> VisualToProxy` and MUST cull the visual + proxy HISMs IN PARALLEL (the corridor/keep-clear cull, `CullCorridorBlockers` `:534`, removes from nav-relevant comps = the proxy, and would ORPHAN the visible tree otherwise); register BOTH the visual and proxy HISMs in `ScatterComponents` so `ClearScatter` reaches them.

- **Radius-aware scatter placement (`FScatterLayer::FootprintRadius`):** new `float FootprintRadius` (0 = auto = `FVector2D(Bounds.BoxExtent.X, .Y).Size() * Scale`). Keep-clear (`IsInKeepClear` `:427`, both direct + mirror path `:258`), the field-edge clamp (`|X|+R > HalfX` / `|Y|+R > HalfY`), and radius-aware `MinSpacing` (`DistSq < (MinSpacing+Ri+Rj)^2`) all consume it so a wide hill centered off-lane no longer sprawls across the corridor (the 14–69-culls/seed root cause). ⚠️ Deriving the radius forces the mesh+scale roll to move BEFORE the keep-clear test, which changes the `FRandomStream` draw order — **existing seeds produce NEW layouts** (documented, NOT a regression; a fixed `OverrideSeed` stays deterministic).

## Arena 10× scale-up & LOD/perf (M7.6)
Added 2026-07-18 (TASK-215; Jonathan-approved 10× MAP SCALE-UP + LOD/PERF directive — plan `C:\Users\wesel\.claude\plans\i-am-a-bit-sunny-bird.md`, OVERWRITTEN since the art-quality version; the plan carries the exact target-number tables and is the numeric source of truth). Executed on branch **`m7.6-arena10x`** (manager relabel of the plan's `m8-arena10x` — GDD M8 = networked 1v1, label collision avoided; batch = M7.6).
- **Branch ownership law:** `m7.6-arena10x` is cut off main BEFORE any batch file change and EXCLUSIVELY owns `Content/Maps/L_Arena.umap` + `DA_BattlefieldScatter` for the batch's duration — M7.5 waves (and any main-lane task) never touch either. The branch is itself the rollback (don't merge = revert). Main's "World axes (arena contract)" ±8,000 numbers remain MAIN's truth; the branch's ±25,000 targets supersede that section only AT MERGE (the section is rewritten then, not before). **OWNERSHIP EXTENSION (manager ruling 2026-07-22, mirrored-mines feature — TASK-253..258):** the branch ownership set TEMPORARILY extends to `GoldNode.h/.cpp`, `MinerUnit.h/.cpp`, `SiegeBotController.h/.cpp`, and `M_GoldGlow.uasset` — all four are FROZEN ON MAIN until the Phase-6 merge (same discipline as L_Arena/DA); the merge inherits the whole mines feature. `SiegePlayerState` stays main-lane (untouched by the feature).
- **Nanite AMENDMENT (Jonathan ruling #4, 2026-07-18 — a SCOPED exception to the "Nanite OFF" ruling in "Textured mesh law", which otherwise stands):** the GAMEPLAY fleet (all SM_<CardID>/SK_<CardID> + scatter gameplay meshes) stays Nanite OFF with classic LODs. VISTA-CLASS meshes ONLY — the Megascans cliff/mountain silhouettes of the far vista ring — get Nanite ON + `bVisibleInRayTracing=false`; vista meshes are NoCollision, `bCanEverAffectNavigation=false`, `CastShadow=false`, never gameplay actors, never inside the play bounds.
- **Classic LOD law:** the SM fleet gets `lod_group='LargeProp'` applied in `Tools/reimport_meshes.py::_reimport_one()` immediately after the Nanite-OFF block (auto 4-LOD chain) — EXCEPT castles, which take explicit reduction LOD1 50% @ screen 0.4 / LOD2 25% @ 0.15 (landmark silhouette). SK units: LOD1 50% @ 0.4 / LOD2 20% @ 0.15 (editor-python `regenerate_lod` for existing rigs; the same step added to `rig_character.py` for future rigs) + `VisibilityBasedAnimTickOption=OnlyTickPoseWhenRendered` and `bEnableUpdateRateOptimizations=true` on `SkeletalVisualMesh`. Pre-approved EMERGENCY lever (perf-watch shortfall only): `SetVisibleInRayTracing(false)` on unit meshes.
- **SEQUENCING LAW (cross-batch, load-bearing):** the reimport LOD-group line lands ON MAIN, QA-passed (TASK-220), BEFORE M7.5's retexture reimport wave (TASK-201/202) runs — that wave then applies LODs for free. TASK-202 carries the blocker.
- **Scatter cull-field naming:** `FScatterLayer` gains `CullStartDistance` / `CullEndDistance` (uu; 0 = never culled) + `bCastShadows`, applied via `SetCullDistances` / `SetCastShadow` in `ResolveComponentForMesh()` and the tree collision-proxy path. Per-layer counts + cull bands + shadow flags are DATA (`DA_BattlefieldScatter`, plan §3 table), not code constants. Hills: no cull, shadows ON (silhouette). Grass/plants: shadows OFF.
- **W1-PREP additions (2026-07-22, Jonathan gate conditions — TASK-249..251):** `FScatterLayer` additionally gains `OverrideMaterial` (`TSoftObjectPtr<UMaterialInterface>`; null = donor materials; applied to the layer's HISM + proxy components — keeps main-lane SM_ assets untouched, the lane-clean route for the hill grass look; the material MUST set `bUsedWithInstancedStaticMeshes=true` — see "Material & Niagara lane laws", instanced-mesh flag law) and hill-surface placement fields `bAllowOnHills` + `MaxPlacementSlopeDeg` (default ~35°; placement traces run AFTER hills exist and accept elevated ground Z; keep-clear/corridor laws unchanged). Hill grass material: `M_HillGrass` (Content/Materials/) — world-aligned TRI-PLANAR / slope-blend matching `M_BattlefieldGround`'s look (grass on walkable faces, rock on steep; straight XY projection smears on slopes — forbidden). Hill scale variance is DA DATA; uniform scale keeps the ≤30° climbability law (angles are scale-invariant), but the MAX scale is CAPPED so the tallest crown stays under the NavMeshBoundsVolume Z (±1,200 today) unless the same task bumps the nav volume + rebuilds nav.
- **Mirrored depleting mines (W1-PREP, 2026-07-22 — TASK-253..258; plan-of-record numbers copied here because the plan file gets overwritten):** 6 neutral mines = 3/side, drawn on the Blue half (|X| ≥ 1,500) then EXACTLY mirrored — **⚠️ AMENDED 2026-08-01: the twin is `(−X, −Y, yaw+180)`, a 180° ROTATION about the map center, NOT the original `(−X, yaw+180)` X-mirror** (the mine twin yaw was already 180, so the change is `Y → −Y`; the 180°-rotational law below governs, and the mine pass is the precedent it generalizes); castle-adjacent GoldNode_Blue/Red DELETED (never ship the 8-node hybrid). AGoldNode becomes neutral/claimable/depleting — its standing laws HOLD (NoCollision, never affects nav, not damageable, no ITeamAgent, soft SM_GoldNode resolve; corridor mines ALLOWED — NoCollision keeps the traversability law safe). **Paired-tunable law:** `AGoldNode::DrainPerMinerPerSecond` ≡ `ASiegePlayerState::MinerGoldPerTick` (both headers cross-note; drift-vs-income tolerance ≤ n gold per depletion documented). **Gold-glow law amended by intensity, not replacement:** `M_GoldGlow` gains scalar param `GlowIntensity` (default = current multiplier ⇒ undriven is byte-identical); the reserve gauge is a lazy MID driving Lerp(0.05, 1.0, Reserve/Initial) — the glows-regardless-of-team law holds. **Seed-order law extends to the mine stream:** dedicated `FRandomStream(Seed XOR 0x4D494E45)`, all mine draws from it in fixed order — layer streams untouched, existing seeds stable. Defaults (tune band recorded): Reserve 300 (band 250–450), spacing 3,000, clearance 600, edge margin 600, slope ≤30°, ≤2× attempts then deterministic fallback slot (the economy never ships short). Hill parity ruling: either-side-has ⇒ both-have (twin hill instance injected on the same HISM; hills are never deleted; trees/rocks in a mine's disc are). TeamFill_Cool_Blue/TeamFill_Warm_Red RectLights are DELETED outright (team mood lighting removed).
- **W-gate law:** every phase boundary is a JONATHAN human perf/feel watch (W1/W2/W3 + the Phase-6 capstone) — machine fps stats are NOT a substitute (the no-machine-stat precedent); nothing past a W-gate dispatches before its sign-off. Play Again ×3 at every editor phase; Message Log clean. Merge to main happens ONLY at the Phase-6 merge gate with Jonathan's sign-off.
- **W1-PREP additions 3 (2026-07-23, Jonathan gate condition "before I approve the W1 playtest" — SHRINK spawn zones to castle-boxes + REMOVE the centerline + ADD a capturable mid zone; TASK-260..264):** the half-field spawn rule (player `bValid && X <= PlacementMaxX` / bot `IsBotHalfPointClear`'s `IsOnOwnHalf` gate) is REPLACED by THREE equal-sized square boxes. Load-bearing details:
  - **Spawn box (per side):** a square centered on the OWNED castle, half-extent `SpawnBoxHalfExtent` (FVector2D, EditDefaultsOnly) default **(840, 840)** = 2× `CastlePlinthClearance` (420) ≈ "twice the castle length × twice the width, 4× footprint area" (the castle footprint is UNSCALED at M7.6 — keep-list; **exact number FLAGGED to Jonathan, tunable**). Player box = around `Castle_Blue` (X<0); bot box = around `Castle_Red` (X>0), each found by the existing team-filtered `TActorIterator<ACastle>`. The box test REPLACES the X-half test ONLY in the SPAWN/PLACEMENT gate — the existing composed checks (navmesh projection, `IsPointInsideCastlePlinth`, slope, obstacle & building clearance) are UNCHANGED and still apply (you place in the ring INSIDE the box but OUTSIDE the plinth). `IsOnOwnHalf`'s OTHER callers (bot miner-approach clamp at ~550/601, own-half unit/hero iteration at ~876/899) are TARGET/APPROACH logic — DO NOT touch them, only the spawn gate. `PlacementMaxX` (the old half-line UPROPERTY, SiegePlayerController.h:527-529, read at .cpp:1236 + logged at :1033) is retired.
  - **Capture zone (mid):** ONE level-placed actor `ACaptureZone` (`CaptureZone.h/.cpp` in `Source/GitClaudeUnrealTest/Siegebound/`, gameplay-actor plain-name law like `ACastle`/`AGoldNode`), instance `CaptureZone_Center` at (0,0,0), half-extent `ZoneHalfExtent` (FVector2D) = the SAME default (840,840) (Jonathan: "same size as the spawnable region on either side"). Straddles X=0. Ownership enum `ECaptureState : uint8 { Neutral, Blue, Red }` (declared in CaptureZone.h) held in member `CaptureOwner` — **NOT** `Owner` (shadow law, AActor::Owner). Initial = Neutral.
  - **Capture rule (symmetric):** every `CaptureEvalInterval` (float, EditDefaultsOnly, default 0.5 s) count friendly vs enemy UNITS inside the box — "unit" = any pawn that is an `ASummonedUnit` (incl. miners) OR `AHeroCharacter`, team via `ITeamAgent::GetTeamId`; buildings/castles/gold-nodes EXCLUDED. A team CAPTURES (CaptureOwner ← that team + broadcast) when it has ≥1 unit inside AND the enemy has 0 inside. Contested (both ≥1) ⇒ CaptureOwner → Neutral (**Contested→Neutral is the LIVE behavior per Jonathan's 2026-07-23 ruling**: `bNeutralizeWhenContested` UPROPERTY DEFAULT **TRUE**; the sticky model — hold the owner until the OTHER side meets the capture condition — survives ONLY as the off-state when the toggle is set false). Empty (both 0) ⇒ NO ownership change (the zone latches the last owner). Null-safe; Play-Again resets CaptureOwner→Neutral (hook the existing match-reset path).
  - **Spawn-enable seam:** player placement valid at a point when (point ∈ own Blue spawn box) **OR** (point ∈ `ACaptureZone` AND CaptureOwner==Blue); bot mirror with Red. Neutral mid ⇒ nobody spawns there. Both controllers read the single `CaptureZone_Center` via `TActorIterator<ACaptureZone>` (null-safe if absent = pre-capture-feature behavior, mid unspawnable).
  - **Delegate:** `FOnCaptureZoneOwnerChanged` (declared in CaptureZone.h) → `UPROPERTY(BlueprintAssignable)` member `OnCaptureOwnerChanged` (delegate law FOn<Owner><Event> / On<Owner><Event>); UI/VFX hook — nothing binds it this pass.
  - **Visual:** the centerline "white line" is the material `M_CenterlineStripe` applied to a level actor (decal/plane) in `L_Arena` — DELETED (build-master locates the actor referencing `M_CenterlineStripe` and removes it; it is NOT in C++). `ACaptureZone` owns a `DecalComponent` that soft-loads `/Game/Materials/M_CaptureZone` (DeferredDecal domain; `M_CenterlineStripe`/`M_SpellReticle` STOCK-NODE recipe, Custom-HLSL ban) and drives a color parameter named exactly `ZoneColor` by owner via MID — Neutral = neutral gray, Blue = `MI_TeamColor_Blue` linear (0.05,0.30,1.00), Red = (1.00,0.10,0.05); null-safe (missing material ⇒ no visual, mechanic still runs, log once). FLAGGED to Jonathan: whether the mid zone needs a visible boundary/owner-tint at all (default: yes — a subtle owner-colored ground decal so the player can see the capturable region + who holds it).
  - **Anchor-clamp law (appendix 3a, 2026-07-23 — TASK-265; the box gate above is UNCHANGED, this governs the DESIRED point fed to it):** shrinking the spawn region to an 840 box did NOT move the bot's spawn ANCHORS, which are still computed castle-relative/target-relative from the pre-shrink geometry (`BotCastleSpawnOffset` 1,750 in front of Castle_Red = X≈23,250 vs a box that starts at X≥24,160; the rule-2a/2b mine anchors sit thousands of uu away). `ComputeValidBotSpawnPoint`'s ring-search tops out at 1,100 uu, so those anchors either pile every wave onto the ONE ring sample that clears the box edge (the observed stack at X 24,032–24,448) or fail outright (rule-2a/2b economy). LAW (**RECONCILED TO THE SHIPPED CODE 2026-07-23** — QA ACCEPTED the TASK-265 flagged deviation, `qa/TASK-265.md`; the shipped behavior IS the law, the earlier box-only wording is superseded): **an ineligible desired anchor is CLAMPED into the NEARER of the two ELIGIBLE regions — the own (Red) castle spawn box, or a Red-OWNED `CaptureZone_Center` — BEFORE the ring-search runs; an already-eligible anchor is passed through untouched.** NOT unconditionally to the castle box. Bot helper `ClampAnchorToBotSpawnRegion(const FVector& Desired) const` — returns `Desired` unchanged when `IsPointInBotSpawnBox(Desired) || IsPointInCapturedZone(Desired)` (the SAME predicate pair the spawn gate uses, so "clamped" and "eligible" can never disagree — De Morgan complement of the gate's test; this pass-through is LOAD-BEARING: it preserves the TASK-262/264-verified behavior that the bot stages at mid while Red owns `CaptureZone_Center`), else builds a BOX candidate (per-axis clamp of ΔX/ΔY about `GetCastleRedLocation()` to `SpawnBoxHalfExtent - SpawnBoxAnchorInset`) and, when a `CaptureZone_Center` exists AND `CanTeamSpawnHere(ETeamId::Red, …)` accepts the zone-clamped point, a ZONE candidate (the same per-axis clamp about the zone origin into `ZoneHalfExtent - SpawnBoxAnchorInset`), and returns whichever is NEARER to `Desired` (strict `<` ⇒ ties break to the box). `Desired.Z` is preserved on both branches; only X/Y are written. **Why the nearer-eligible-region rule and not box-only (QA-verified rationale, load-bearing — keep it):** (a) the zone branch is INERT with no zone placed / a Neutral zone / a Blue-owned zone — all three return the box-clamped point, byte-identical to the original box-only wording; (b) castle-relative anchors are ALWAYS nearer the box at the shipped geometry (≈950 uu vs ≈22,450 uu), so M7.6 ruling #1 ("spawn castle-front and MARCH") and rule-4 attack-wave behavior are untouched — ONLY rule-2 ECONOMY anchors can ever select the zone branch, and an *attack* unit appearing at mid is therefore a DEFECT to report, never this law; (c) a box-only clamp would DELETE TASK-264 PIE result (f) BY CONSTRUCTION — rule-2a's anchor is X-clamped to 0 by `IsOnOwnHalf` and the 1,100-uu ring reaches the ±840 zone, whereas box-only moves that anchor to X≈24,200, from which mid is unreachable — and would leave the bot structurally unable to use a zone it owns while the player can (dead code on the bot half of the capture feature). Self-healing: if the zone flips owner mid-match the branch goes false on the next call and the clamp reverts to the box — no latch. **Accepted consequence:** with Red HOLDING the zone, rule 2b may anchor a Deep Mine INSIDE the mid zone (buildings are exempt from `UnitSpawnClearance` but not from the clamp) — legal (a held zone is an eligible spawn region) and design-consistent (raidable mid-field economy); recorded as board FLAGGED item (iv), W1 WATCH, no code change. `SpawnBoxAnchorInset` (float, EditDefaultsOnly, default **40** uu) keeps the clamped anchor off the exact box edge so the ring-search has room on both sides. The plinth is NOT special-cased in the clamp — the existing ring walk-out owns it. `BotCastleSpawnOffset` / `BotSpawnLaneSpread` / the two `IsOnOwnHalf` anchor clamps are KEPT AS AUTHORED (the clamp dominates them at an 840 box; they re-activate untouched if the box ever grows) — M7.6 ruling #1's "spawn castle-front and MARCH" intent survives: the clamped anchor is still the centerline-facing front band of the castle box.
  - **Unit spawn clearance (anti-stacking, appendix 3a):** `UnitSpawnClearance` (float, EditDefaultsOnly, default **150** uu, `ClampMin 0`) — a NON-building bot spawn point is rejected while any live `ASummonedUnit` (either team) is within that 2D distance, checked inside `IsBotHalfPointClear` next to the existing `BuildingClearance` block (units get the unit rule, buildings keep the building rule). This is what forces the deterministic ring-search to walk to a FREE slot instead of re-serving the same point to every wave. 0 disables. The player-side placement path is deliberately NOT given this rule this pass (Jonathan just approved that behavior; a player-side stack is a separate, unreported concern).
  - **Spawn-Z law (appendix 3a):** bot spawn float is diagnosed before it is fixed. `bLogSpawnZDiagnostic` (bool, EditDefaultsOnly, default **true** for W1) emits ONE `LogGitClaudeUnrealTest` Log line per bot unit spawn with the chosen point Z, the traced ground Z and the delta — never on `LogSiegeBot` (the one-line-per-FIRED-rule decision-trace law is inviolate). Only if a material residual float (> ~50 uu) survives de-stacking AND is attributable to the nav-projected Z may the bot-only ground snap be added: `SnapPointToGround(const FVector& Point) const` (downward `ECC_WorldStatic` trace, the `BattlefieldScatter::GroundZAt` precedent) applied to the FINAL OutPoint, and it may only LOWER the point by at most `MaxGroundSnapDrop` (float, EditDefaultsOnly, default **120** uu) — the cap exists because scatter hills leave `ECC_WorldStatic` on Ignore, so an uncapped snap would drop a unit through a hill to the ground plane. The SHARED `ASiegePlayerController::SpawnUnitSwarm` capsule lift is OFF LIMITS (it is the player's path too); a residual traced to it or to the tall-Ogre capsule stays on the existing Ogre-near-hill spawn-lift WATCH.
  - **Lane / ownership extension (manager ruling 2026-07-23):** ALL branch-lane. Branch ownership extends further to `SiegePlayerController.{h,cpp}` (SiegeBotController.{h,cpp} was ALREADY frozen by the mines feature) + the new `CaptureZone.{h,cpp}` + `M_CaptureZone` + the `CaptureZone_Center` L_Arena instance + the centerline-actor deletion — all FROZEN on main until the Phase-6 merge (the merge inherits the whole spawn-box/capture feature; same discipline as the mines-feature extension). `SiegePlayerState` stays main-lane untouched.

## Unit commands (Shield Wall stances) — ATTACK / HOLD / DEFEND (W1, 2026-07-23) — ⚠️ PARTIALLY SUPERSEDED 2026-07-27 by "Group orders — 3-zone HOLD + AMBUSH" (next section): the one-circle HOLD stance flow is REPLACED; ATTACK/DEFEND stances, the enum, and the latched-stance law (now scoped to STANCES only) SURVIVE
Added 2026-07-23 (Jonathan directive: *"press certain buttons to make the units around you do certain actions, similar to 'Shield Wall'"* — 3 commands on T/R/E). **W1-SCOPED: develops on `m7.6-arena10x` and INTEGRATES into the W1 build** (NOT held like the M7.7 deck-details chain). Applies ONLY to `ECardProfile::Standard` **PLAYER (Blue)** units — Siege (Ogre/Sapper), Support (Cleric), and None (miners) bodies are UNTOUCHED; bot/Red Standard units keep the legacy body byte-for-byte.

- **Command enum:** `ESiegeUnitCommand : uint8 { Attack, Hold, Defend }` — declared **header-only** in `Source/GitClaudeUnrealTest/Siegebound/UnitCommand.h` (the `TeamId.h` pure-data-type exception; BOTH the player controller and the unit include it, so it must NOT live inside either heavy header).
- **Command STATE home = `ASiegePlayerController`** (per-PLAYER, player-issued — the RTS-command-on-the-controller pattern; the T/R/E bindings + the ground reticle already live here; M8-ready since each networked player's own controller owns its command, where `ASiegeGameState` would need per-team arrays and is flagged local-only-through-M7). Members: `ESiegeUnitCommand CurrentCommand` (transient, default `Attack`); `bool bHasIssuedCommand` (transient, default **false** — WHILE false the units run the LEGACY body, so behavior is byte-identical to today until the player first presses a key, Q3 default); `FVector HoldLocation` (transient); `float HoldRadius` (EditDefaultsOnly, default **1500** uu — the "within that location" attack radius around the hold point; ~1000 suggested by the directive, raised for the M7.6 10× arena — FLAGGED tunable Q1). Getters `GetCurrentCommand()` / `GetHoldLocation()` / `GetHoldRadius()` / `HasIssuedCommand()` (BlueprintPure). Setter `SetUnitCommand(ESiegeUnitCommand)` latches the stance + `bHasIssuedCommand=true` + broadcasts. Delegate `FOnUnitCommandChanged` → `UPROPERTY(BlueprintAssignable) OnUnitCommandChanged` (delegate law `FOn<Owner><Event>`/`On<Owner><Event>`; carries the new `ESiegeUnitCommand` as a byte) — the HUD binds it; nothing else must.
- **Input:** three NEW input actions `IA_CmdAttack` / `IA_CmdHold` / `IA_CmdDefend` (Content/Input/Actions/, `/Game/Input/Actions/IA_Cmd*`, Digital/bool trigger), MAPPED in the EXISTING `IMC_Hero` (`/Game/Input/IMC_Hero` — the same context that carries `IA_Card1..6` + `IA_Rally`, applied by `AHeroCharacter` at priority 1) to keys **T (Attack) / R (Hold) / E (Defend)**. ⚠ **E/R/T CONFLICT CHECK (manager, 2026-07-23):** the ONLY letter-key gameplay binding today is **Rally = Q** (`HeroCharacter.cpp:182`); cards are 1–6, Attack = LMB, Jump = Space, Sprint = Shift, UICursor = Left Alt, Cancel = RMB/Esc. **E/R/T READ AS FREE** — but `IMC_Hero` is a BINARY `.uasset` the manager cannot text-verify, so the art-director MUST open `IMC_Hero` in-editor and CONFIRM no existing E/R/T mapping before adding, and FLAG (never silently stomp) any conflict. Controller soft-refs `CmdAttackActionAsset` / `CmdHoldActionAsset` / `CmdDefendActionAsset` (`TSoftObjectPtr<UInputAction>`, resolved null-safe by the existing `ResolveInputAction` pattern — a missing asset logs once, that key is inert, never a crash) → resolved `CmdAttackAction` etc. → bound `ETriggerEvent::Started` to `OnCmdAttackPressed` / `OnCmdHoldPressed` / `OnCmdDefendPressed`.
- **HOLD location pick (reuses the spell reticle):** pressing R enters a ground-target mode that REUSES the existing targeting-reticle machinery (`SpawnSpellReticle` / soft `M_SpellReticle` decal / the ground trace) — LMB confirms the point (→ `SetUnitCommand(Hold)` + `HoldLocation` = the traced surface point), RMB/Esc cancels (no stance change). New members `bInHoldTargetMode` + `BeginHoldTarget()` / `ConfirmHoldTarget()` / `CancelHoldTarget()`; MUST be mutually exclusive with spell targeting (`bInTargetingMode`) and placement mode — entering one cancels the others. Null-safe (missing `M_SpellReticle` ⇒ no visual, the pick still works off the trace).
- **Enemy spawn-box exposure (ATTACK priority) = on `ACastle`:** the "within the spawn of the enemy castle" test lives on the castle (castle-centered, and the unit already holds the enemy-castle pointer via `FindNearestEnemyCastle`). Add `FVector2D ACastle::SpawnBoxHalfExtent` (EditDefaultsOnly, default **(840, 840)**) + `bool ACastle::IsPointInSpawnBox(const FVector& Point) const` (BlueprintPure; `|Point.X−ActorX| ≤ HalfX && |Point.Y−ActorY| ≤ HalfY`). **PAIRED-TUNABLE LAW (3-way):** `ACastle::SpawnBoxHalfExtent` ≡ `ASiegePlayerController::SpawnBoxHalfExtent` ≡ `ASiegeBotController::SpawnBoxHalfExtent` (all default (840,840)); each header cross-notes the other two. This does NOT touch the bot's existing `IsPointInBotSpawnBox` (TASK-262 logic UNDISTURBED) — it is an additive third reader; a future refactor MAY have both controllers delegate to the castle helper (OUT of scope, flagged).
- **Standard-body reshaping (`ASummonedUnit::UpdateState`, the M1/M2 body ONLY):** the Siege/Support/None dispatch at the top is UNCHANGED. A player-team gate reads the command: `Team == the local human player's team (Blue through M7; the single ASiegePlayerController)` **AND** `PC->HasIssuedCommand()`; otherwise the LEGACY body runs byte-for-byte (bot/Red units, and player units pre-first-command). New unit members/helpers: `float DefendRadius` (EditDefaultsOnly, default **2500** uu — "attacking the castle" = enemy within this 2D radius of the OWN castle, Q6 default; FLAGGED tunable), `AcquireEnemyNearPoint(const FVector& Anchor, float Radius) const` (AcquireTarget's team-filtered iteration anchored on an arbitrary point instead of AggroRadius-from-self), `FindNearestEnemyInSpawnBox(const ACastle* EnemyCastle) const`, `FindOwnCastle() const` (mirror of `FindNearestEnemyCastle`, `Team == Team`), and `EnterAdvanceToLocation(const FVector& Point)` (mirror of `EnterAdvance` calling `AI->MoveToLocation` — HOLD marches to a POINT, not an actor; the existing `EnterAdvance(AActor*)` is unchanged and still serves DEFEND's own-castle goal + ATTACK's castle/target goals).
  - **ATTACK (T):** local self-defense `AcquireTarget()` is UNCHANGED; the no-aggro march GOAL is the STABLE enemy castle (`Goal = CurrentTarget ?? FindNearestEnemyCastle()`) — byte-identical to the legacy Standard body's castle-kill march, so Blue Standard units march the full 10× field and `EnterAttack` Castle_Red. **RETIRED (TASK-282, 2026-07-24):** the spawn-box-defenders-FIRST substitution (`FindNearestEnemyInSpawnBox`) and its `EnemyBaseEngageRadius` gate (TASK-275/280/281) are REMOVED — box-first against a continuously-respawning bot box meant the castle was never attacked (final-approach halt); box defenders that enter `AggroRadius`(600) on the approach are still engaged first via the unchanged `AcquireTarget`/`CurrentTarget` precedence. (⚠ retiring the original TASK-275 "box-defenders FIRST" spec is a DESIGN CHANGE flagged for Jonathan's call.)
  - **HOLD (R):** `CurrentTarget = AcquireEnemyNearPoint(HoldLocation, HoldRadius)` (only enemies inside the hold disc are eligible; a target that leaves the disc is dropped). `Goal = CurrentTarget ?? HoldLocation` via `EnterAdvanceToLocation`; on arrival with no in-disc enemy the unit holds (`EnterIdle`).
  - **DEFEND (E):** `OwnCastle = FindOwnCastle()`; `CurrentTarget = AcquireEnemyNearPoint(OwnCastle→Location, DefendRadius)` (only enemies within the defend disc of the own castle). `Goal = CurrentTarget ?? OwnCastle` (own castle is an actor → `EnterAdvance`). Units fall back toward home and only fight castle attackers.
- **Persistence/scope (Q2 default = YES latched global stance):** a command is a LATCHED player STANCE applying to all currently-alive AND future-spawned player Standard units until another command replaces it — units read the LIVE controller state each `UpdateState`, so newly-spawned units adopt the current stance automatically. Play Again resets `CurrentCommand→Attack` + `bHasIssuedCommand→false` + `HoldLocation→ZeroVector` (hook the existing match-reset path).
- **HUD indicator (optional, fast-follow — Q5):** an on-screen active-command readout (`CommandIndicatorText` on `WBP_HUD`, bound to `OnUnitCommandChanged`) + an optional HOLD-point ground marker. NON-blocking for the W1 integration (gameplay ships without it); FLAGGED for Jonathan to pull in-scope now or defer.
- **Bot (Q4 default = player-only):** bot/Red units are UNCHANGED — the directive says "the units around **you**". No bot command AI this pass.
- **Lane / ownership (manager ruling 2026-07-23):** ALL branch-lane on `m7.6-arena10x`. `SiegePlayerController.{h,cpp}` is ALREADY branch-frozen (spawn-box feature); the freeze set EXTENDS to `Castle.{h,cpp}` + `SummonedUnit.{h,cpp}` + the new `UnitCommand.h` + the 3 `IA_Cmd*` assets + the `IMC_Hero` mapping edit for this feature's duration (frozen on main until the Phase-6 merge; same discipline as the spawn-box/capture + mines extensions). Integration is NOT held — this folds into the W1 build.

## Group orders — 3-zone HOLD + AMBUSH (2026-07-27, TASK-344..346)
Added 2026-07-27 (Jonathan's approved two-lane plan, design authority `C:\Users\wesel\.claude\plans\groovy-bouncing-manatee.md` — verified against the working tree with file:line citations). The one-circle HOLD stance is REPLACED by per-unit **GROUP ORDERS** with a 3-stage circle pick flow (SELECT units → POSITION zone → ATTACK zone, mouse-wheel resizable), and a new **AMBUSH** command joins on **F**. Develops on **main** (the W1 branch freeze above is historical — M7.6 merged).
- **SUPERSESSION (exact):** DELETED — the team-wide HOLD stance flow: `HoldRadius` / `HoldLocation`, `bInHoldTargetMode`, `Begin/Update/Confirm/CancelHoldTarget` (as-built SiegePlayerController.cpp:1994-2167). SURVIVES byte-identical — `ESiegeUnitCommand : uint8 { Attack, Hold, Defend }` (**WBP_HUD's switch pins depend on the byte layout; the `Hold` member STAYS DECLARED but the team-wide Hold stance becomes unreachable**), `SetUnitCommand` / `FOnUnitCommandChanged` / the HUD stance display, the ATTACK (T) and DEFEND (E) stance branches, the latched-stance law (**now scoped to STANCES only** — group orders are per-unit assignments, not latched team stances), bot/Red untouched, and the Siege/Support/miner exclusion law.
- **Types (naming law):** `UnitCommand.h` (the header-only pure-data home) gains **`ESiegeGroupCommandType : uint8 { Hold, Ambush }`** and **`FSiegeUnitGroup`** (group id, type, `PositionCenter`/`PositionRadius`, `AttackCenter`/`AttackRadius`, weak-ptr `Members`, two marker-decal refs). Controller (`ASiegePlayerController`): private **`EGroupPickStage : uint8 { None, Select, Position, AttackZone }`**, **`TArray<FSiegeUnitGroup> UnitGroups`** (multiple concurrent groups; re-selected units are STOLEN from older groups; emptied groups + markers destroyed, ≤1 s prune), **`FindUnitGroup(int32)`**, ONE teardown call **`CancelGroupPick()`** (melee-release-before-early-out law) swapped into ALL 8 existing teardown sites, **`SpawnGroupCircleDecal`** (reticle-recipe clone: identity spawn → absolute −90 pitch — the TASK-100 composition lesson; `DecalSize=(500,R,R)`; shared `M_SpellReticle`, null-safe). Unit (`ASummonedUnit`): **`int32 CommandGroupId`** + precomputed **`FVector GroupStationOffset`** (per-unit scalars ONLY — no arrays on units; a null `FindUnitGroup` result SELF-HEALS to the legacy stance gate), **`UpdateStateGrouped`** dispatched ABOVE the stance gate, public **`IsGroupCommandEligible()`** (Standard + Blue + alive + unfrozen — `Profile` is private; this is the new public API). Delegate **`FOnCommandPromptChanged`** → `UPROPERTY(BlueprintAssignable) OnCommandPromptChanged` (FString param — widget rule; prompts also LOG, so gameplay ships without the BP edit).
- **Tunables (EditDefaultsOnly, ALL flagged for Jonathan's feel-pass):** `GroupRadiusWheelStep` **100** · `GroupRadiusMin` **200** · `GroupRadiusMax` **5000** · stage defaults `GroupSelectRadiusDefault` **1200** / `GroupPositionRadiusDefault` **700** / `GroupAttackRadiusDefault` **1500**.
- **Wheel law: POLLED input, NO new InputAction.** `WasInputKeyJustPressed(EKeys::MouseScrollUp/Down)` inside the pick branch of `PlayerTick` (the same polling pattern LMB/RMB/Esc already use there). The wheel is verified globally unbound (no camera zoom exists) and must stay INERT outside the pick flow.
- **Input asset:** ONE new **`IA_CmdAmbush`** (`/Game/Input/Actions/`, Digital/bool) mapped to **F** in the existing `/Game/Input/IMC_Hero`. F is verified free in exploration, but `IMC_Hero` is a binary asset — the art-director MUST confirm F unmapped in-editor and FLAG (never stomp) any conflict. Controller soft-ref + null-safe resolve (missing asset ⇒ F inert, never a crash — the `IA_Cmd*` pattern).
- **Behavior law:** priority ladder = enemies in ATTACK zone → else enemies in POSITION zone → else walk to the per-unit station and wait. **Anti-thrash STICKINESS** (the TASK-280/282 freeze lesson): keep the current target while it is alive AND zone-valid; acquire only when target-less; single MONOTONE exception — a position-tier target upgrades to an attack-zone target when one appears (cannot oscillate). **HOLD leash:** drop the target the tick it exits BOTH zones (disengage + return). **AMBUSH leash-exemption:** the zone drop-test is SKIPPED while a live target exists — finish the kill, then the ladder resumes. **Squad spread:** deterministic golden-angle sunflower station offsets inside the position circle, computed ONCE at confirm, nav-projected (kills mill-at-one-point); arrival = the existing 150 uu tolerance; **the TASK-275 kite-fix is UNTOUCHED** and covers chase→return. **Release:** any new command (T/E/new R/F) replaces membership; **T/E clears ALL groups**; death clears; Play-Again resets. **Markers:** at final confirm the dropped Position + Attack circles TRANSFER to the group as persistent markers (delivers the TASK-276-deferred hold marker); markers die with their group.
- **Byte-identical set (regression law):** the legacy post-gate stance body, `AcquireTarget`, Siege/Support/miner paths, bot + all Red units, ATTACK/DEFEND branches, placement + spell targeting, `SetUnitCommand`/HUD stance display, all combat/economy/match-flow.
- **Compile traps (restate in every dispatch touching these files):** no literal `*/` inside doc comments; `FString::Printf` format strings literal/`constexpr` (UE 5.8 TCheckedFormatString — the TASK-268 C7595 lesson).
- **AMBUSH-vs-building semantics — FLAGGED to Jonathan, default ships:** buildings are valid ambush targets and "finish the kill" = besiege until destroyed; his feel-pass may override.

## Networked 1v1 (M8) — authority, replication + RPC law (2026-07-28)
Added 2026-07-28 (M8 kickoff, GDD §9.8; Jonathan: *"If it is ok to run it in parallel, go ahead and get started on M8"*). Binding on every M8 task and on ALL future gameplay code (single-player code written after this date must not add new violations).
- **AUTHORITY MODEL (manager ruling, flagged default):** LISTEN SERVER — the host is server + Blue player; the joining client is Red. **Strictly server-authoritative gameplay** per GDD §9.8: gold, spawns, combat, spells, upgrades, commands all mutate ONLY on the authority; clients request via RPC and render replicated state. Dedicated servers, OnlineSubsystem/EOS/Steam sessions, matchmaking and accounts are OUT OF SCOPE this milestone (GDD §10) — join flow = LAN/direct-IP (`open <ip>` / subsystem-wrapped travel).
- **RPC naming law (UE convention, now house law):** `Server<Verb><Noun>` (Reliable, `WithValidation` on every input-bearing server RPC), `Client<Verb><Noun>`, `Multicast<Verb><Noun>`. Every gameplay mutation a player initiates moves behind a `Server*` RPC on the OWNING controller (card play, placement confirm, spell target, discard, group-order confirm, upgrade purchase). Cosmetic-only effects may multicast; never gameplay state.
- **Replication law:** replicated properties registered in `GetLifetimeReplicatedProps` (`DOREPLIFETIME`/`_CONDITION`); client-side reactions in `OnRep_<Property>` handlers (exact naming; **RATIFIED at the TASK-353 sign-off: bool properties drop the `b` prefix in the handler name — `bDestroyed` → `OnRep_Destroyed`**); `HasAuthority()` guards at every mutation site (a mutation without a guard is a QA FAIL in M8 code); visual client-side systems (MIDs, decals, widget fills) DRIVE from OnReps, never from client-computed gameplay state. **Net log category: `LogSiegeNet`** (the `LogSiege<Domain>` law; declared/defined in `SiegeSessionSubsystem.{h,cpp}`, usable by any M8 code — both P1 lanes compile together at integration). **The P1 architecture is SIGNED (2026-07-28): `handoffs/TASK-353-architecture.md` D1–D14 — on any conflict between a board summary and the signed doc, the doc wins.**
- **TEAM LAW (retires "Blue = local"):** team derives from PlayerState/registration at PostLogin — host = Blue, client = Red (M8 default). **`GetFirstPlayerController()` is BANNED in gameplay code** — the biggest shipped offender is the group-orders polling (units read the FIRST controller's `UnitGroups`, `3068286`); the replacement pattern is the unit's OWNING TEAM controller resolve (designed at TASK-353). Single-player ("Play vs Bot", standalone NetMode) must remain BYTE-IDENTICAL in behavior — authority is everywhere true there.
- **Net class naming:** session plumbing in `USiegeSessionSubsystem` (UGameInstanceSubsystem, new files `SiegeSessionSubsystem.{h,cpp}`) — `HostListenMatch()` / `JoinMatch(const FString& Address)` / `LeaveMatch()`. Session/menu widget: `USessionMenuWidget` ↔ `/Game/UI/WBP_SessionMenu` (the `U<Name>Widget` ↔ `WBP_<Name>` law; fresh-built, NEVER duplicate+reparent). Existing "Play vs Bot" flow is PRESERVED — the bot remains as practice mode (flagged default).
- **⚖️ NET RELEVANCY LAW (added 2026-07-29 — the P1 two-client gate failure: match-critical state replicated under UE's DEFAULT 150 m relevancy inside a 500 m arena ⇒ scatter never replicated (client 0 gold nodes vs host 6) and the FAR castle's HP/crumble/destroyed never replicated (488 m: host 500 / client 2000; the near castle at 12 m was fine). The OnRep code was correct — relevancy was the defect. THREE TIERS, and EVERY replicated class DECLARES its tier:**
  - **TIER A — `bAlwaysRelevant = true`: match-critical singletons/near-singletons whose truth must not depend on where a camera is.** Fixed, tiny population (≤ ~10 actors), low-frequency event-driven state ⇒ bandwidth is negligible. P1 membership: GameState-class, PlayerState-class (**engine already sets these — VERIFY and document, do not blind-set**), **both `ACastle`s**, **`ASiegeBattlefieldScatter`**. A singleton that drives world generation or a win condition is ALWAYS Tier A.
  - **TIER B — ARENA-SCALED `NetCullDistanceSquared`, derived from ONE named constant, never a per-class literal.** For actors that are gameplay-relevant anywhere in the arena but numerous. The constant is `SiegeNet::ArenaRelevancyDistance` (one-concept header `SiegeNetLimits.h`, the `TeamId.h` precedent) = full arena diagonal × 1.1 — **currently 60,000 uu ⇒ `NetCullDistanceSquared` 3.6e9**; when the arena grows again (M7.6 may), ONE constant changes and every Tier-B class follows. **This is exactly why option (b) alone was rejected: a per-class literal is a drift trap that every future class must remember.** P1 membership: `AHeroCharacter` (a player's own pawn is owner-relevant regardless; the ENEMY hero must read across the arena).
  - **TIER C — engine default relevancy:** purely local/cosmetic actors (VFX, props) with no gameplay truth to carry.
