## ⚖️ THE FINE-TUNE RUNG — QLoRA on `Qwen3-4B` (2026-08-03) — **JONATHAN'S RESERVED RUNG, OPENED BY JONATHAN**

Added 2026-08-03 (manager, law written FIRST as always; batch **FINE-TUNE**, TASK-476+). Design authority = the approved plan `C:\Users\wesel\.claude\plans\inherited-bubbling-acorn.md` — **the plan file wins over any board summary or clause here**; it carries the context, the six stages, the pre-registered decision rules, the nine gate clauses, the verification table and the ten-item trap ledger, and this section does not re-derive them.

### 1. ⛔ PROVENANCE — READ THIS BEFORE ANYTHING ELSE, BECAUSE THE RUNG WAS EXPLICITLY RESERVED AND SOMEBODY WILL LATER READ THIS BATCH AS AN AGENT OVERREACHING

> ### ⛔ **§12f RESERVED RUNG 3 TO JONATHAN BY NAME — *"THE NEXT RUNG IS JONATHAN'S AND HE RESERVED IT"* — AND `TASKBOARD.md`'s LLM-ASSISTANT flag (g) / the TASK-428 spec BOTH FORBID AN AGENT PROPOSING IT. ⇒ ✅ **HE HAS EXERCISED IT HIMSELF.** THE RESERVATION WAS HONOURED, NOT BYPASSED.**

- ⚖️ **Both sentences are true at once and both stay on the record: *no agent may propose this rung* — unchanged, still binding for the NEXT one — and *Jonathan opened this one*.** ⛔ **A future agent may not cite this batch as precedent for proposing a rung.** The precedent this batch sets is about **how a rung is executed once opened**, never about who may open it.
- **JONATHAN'S FOUR RULINGS (DECIDED 2026-08-03 — no agent re-opens them), recorded as HIS:**
  1. **THE FINE-TUNE RUNG IS OPENED.** §12f reserved it to him; he exercised it.
  2. **CUDA IS AMENDED, NOT WAIVED** — scoped to the shipped runtime backend; host-side at authoring time only. **The amendment and its four checkable boundaries live in §6 of "In-match LLM command assistant", edited IN PLACE.** ⛔ Not recorded as an exception.
  3. **TEACHER = AN OPEN PERMISSIVE MODEL (Apache/MIT) ONLY; no commercial-API outputs anywhere in the training data.** **The full duty lives in §7's licensing clause**, beside the banned-models list, because it is that rule one level up.
  4. **JONATHAN AUTHORS THE SENTENCES AND REVIEWS 100 % OF THE REFUSE/CLARIFY GOLDS** (~100–150 decisions). Execute rows are drafted and **20 %-sampled.**

### 2. ⛔⭐ THE OUTCOME LAW — A WAVE THAT ENDS AT STOP 1 IS THE **BEST AVAILABLE RESULT**, AND THIS IS WRITTEN **BEFORE** THE MEASUREMENT RUNS, NOT AFTER

> ### ⭐ **THE CHEAPEST POSSIBLE OUTCOME OF THIS BATCH IS DISCOVERING THE SHIPPED LANE WAS NEVER MEASURED AT ITS OWN BEST. FIXING D1 IS HOURS. QLoRA IS WEEKS. ⇒ A BATCH THAT STOPS AFTER STAGE B, HAVING FIXED THE WIRE FORMAT AND SAVED WEEKS, IS A ***SUCCESS*** AND IS REPORTED AS ONE.**

- ⛔ **NO HANDOFF, QA REPORT, BOARD ENTRY OR SLACK POST MAY FRAME AN EARLY STOP AS A FAILURE, A CANCELLATION, OR "the fine-tune didn't happen".** It is the measurement doing its job. **The reason this clause is dated BEFORE the run is that an early stop is only credible as a success if it was declared one in advance** — declared afterwards it reads as consolation, which is exactly how a pipeline learns to push past its own stops.
- ⚖️ **THIS IS §12f's LESSON APPLIED FORWARD.** *"This rung is done"*, bought with three measured mechanisms, was recorded there as **a RESULT and not a defeat**. The same standing applies here, one rung up.
- ⚠️ **AND THE SYMMETRIC TRAP IS NAMED TOO: this clause is NOT a licence to stop early to avoid hard work.** The stops are **pre-registered and conditional on a measured comparison** (§4). *"It felt like the wire format was the problem"* is not STOP 1. **A stop is a number, in writing, against a rule written before the run.**

### 3. ⚠️ THE THREE DIVERGENCES — **THE MEASURED LANE IS NOT THE SHIPPED LANE**, IN THREE INDEPENDENT PLACES

Each found separately, by reading the **shipped** path rather than the measured one. **This is §12a's two-lane hole (`DA_AssistantVocabulary`) recurring three more times** — same shape, different seam: *the lanes agree on everything a reader can check and differ in content the reader never sees.*

| # | Divergence | Consequence |
|---|---|---|
| **D1** | **CHAT TEMPLATE.** The eval harness wraps the prompt in Qwen3's chat template (Zone A as `system`, B+C as `user`, `add_generation_prompt=true`); `USiegeAssistantComponent::ComposeTurnPrompt` **concatenates raw, no roles.** | ⛔ **Every number on record — 18 → 19 → 20, the 66.7 % — describes a prompt shape THE GAME NEVER PRODUCES.** Qwen3 is post-trained on its template, so raw concat is off-distribution **by construction.** |
| **D2** | **ROSTER COLLAPSE.** Shipped Zone C caps at `MaxRosterKinds = 8` and collapses the tail; the harness prints all **13**, on `t0`. | ⛔ **The sealed `holdout2` may be authored against a board the game does not render, inside the block the model reads unit names from.** |
| **D3** | **SPARSE BOARDS ARE STRUCTURALLY UNMEASURABLE.** `VerifyFixtureKindParity` enforces every fixture carry exactly `SpikeRoster`'s 13 kinds, and the GBNF is built from `SpikeRoster` rather than the fixture. | The seam where Zone A's few-shots name `footman`/`sorcerer`/`archer` on a board **lacking them** cannot be measured by the only instrument that exists. |

- ⛔ **D2 IS AN ESCALATION TO JONATHAN, NOT A TASK DECISION.** All three candidate fixes move something **frozen**: shrink the fixtures (⛔ `t0`'s bytes are sealed law, §12a + §16), teach the harness to collapse (same), or raise `MaxRosterKinds` to 13 (⛔ §12c freezes Zone B/C bytes this wave). ⚠️ **It may mean `holdout2` is no longer authored against the thing it would score** — which is a question about **Jonathan's one honest measurement**, and nobody else may spend it. **The escalation carries M3's byte diff as its evidence.**

- ✅⚖️ **D2 IS RESOLVED — 2026-08-04, BY JONATHAN, VIA THE THIRD OPTION. THE ESCALATION WAS RAISED CORRECTLY, IT WAS HELD CORRECTLY, AND HE SPENT IT.**
  > ### ⭐ **`MaxRosterKinds` 8 → **13** (ALL THIRTEEN COMMANDABLE KINDS). HIS RULING, ASKED AND ANSWERED WITH THE COST IN FRONT OF HIM.**
  - ⭐ **AND THE DIRECTION IS THE GOOD ONE, WHICH IS WHY IT IS WORTH SAYING PLAINLY: THIS *SHRINKS* D2, IT DOES NOT TRADE IT.** The harness already prints **13**; the shipped builder printed **8**. **Raising the cap CONVERGES the two lanes on `t0` instead of moving one of them away from the other** — the shipped builder then emits 13 roster rows and computes `other_kinds: none`, which is byte-identical to the harness's hardcoded `none`. ⇒ ⛔ **`t0`'s SEALED BYTES WERE NOT TOUCHED, THE HARNESS WAS NOT TOUCHED, AND THE FIXTURE WAS NOT SHRUNK.** The only thing that moved is the lane that was *wrong about the game*.
  - ✅ ⇒ **STOP 2 (THE ROSTER SEAM) IS DISCHARGED.** Its condition was *"if M3's artifact diff fails on the roster block, resolve D2 first."* **D2 is resolved before M3 runs.** ⚠️ **STOP 1 (the wire format / chat template, D1) IS UNAFFECTED AND STILL STANDS** — nothing here touches `ComposeTurnPrompt`'s raw concatenation, and a reader who takes this as *"the divergences are handled"* has conflated two independent findings.
  - ⚠️ **THE COST HE WAS TOLD AND ACCEPTED, RECORDED AS A NAMED RISK RATHER THAN AS A FOOTNOTE: ~887 CHARS AGAINST AN 893-CHAR ZONE-C BUDGET — ABOUT 6 CHARACTERS OF HEADROOM.** He accepted that **a future 14th unit kind forces a re-tune.** ⇒ **See `AS-§20.3` for the risk entry, the two things that make it survivable, and the ONE honest lever (`ZoneBCharReserve`) that buys ~124 chars back — which is boarded as a MEASURE-FIRST task and ⛔ must never be taken from a derived figure.**
  - ⚖️ **§12c's *"ZONE B AND ZONE C BYTES DO NOT MOVE IN THIS WAVE"* IS SUPERSEDED FOR THIS CONSTANT AND FOR THIS CONSTANT ONLY.** A wave-scoped freeze cannot outrank the user's own ruling on the thing the freeze was protecting a measurement of. ⇒ **The `t0` tripwire `zoneC_chars = 887` is RETIRED as a shipped-lane invariant** (it was measured on the **fixture**, which prints 13 and always did — see §8's fixture-vs-builder qualifier); **the SHIPPED builder's `zoneC_chars` on `t0` moves from 670 toward 887 and that is the two lanes AGREEING.** ⛔ **Any bar re-measured after this change is not comparable to a bar measured before it, and the handoff says so.**

**⚖️ D2's EXACT SHAPE — MEASURED FROM BOTH LANES' SOURCE 2026-08-03 BY THE MANAGER, FIRST-HAND, AND IT CORRECTS A RELAYED CLAIM.** A relayed finding reached me as *"M3's diff will show a KEY-SET difference, not merely a truncated value list."* ⛔ **THAT IS FALSE ON THE ARTIFACT, and it is corrected here rather than carried:**

| lane | on `t0` (13 kinds) | source |
|---|---|---|
| **spike / harness** | 13 roster rows **+ `other_kinds: none`** — ⛔ **HARDCODED, not computed** | `SiegeLlamaSpike.cpp:632` (`Out += TEXT("other_kinds: none\n");`, with the comment *"Nothing collapses in EITHER fixture by construction"*) |
| **shipped builder** | 8 roster rows **+ `other_kinds: 5 kinds, N units`** — **computed** | `SiegeAssistantSnapshot.cpp:1313-1320` |

- ✅ **THE KEY IS PRESENT IN BOTH LANES. The key SET is identical** — `places, roster, other_kinds, stances, hero, pending, order`, and both files carry that fixed-key comment. ⇒ **The diff is (a) FIVE ROSTER ROWS MISSING from shipped, and (b) the `other_kinds:` VALUE — `none` vs `5 kinds, N units`.** ⚠️ **Same key, different value, plus absent rows. Board that expectation, not a key-set difference, or the first reader chases a defect that is not there.**
- ⚖️ **AND THE SHARPER FINDING, WHICH IS WORTH MORE THAN THE CORRECTION: THE SPIKE'S `other_kinds:` IS A CONSTANT, SO THE SPIKE LANE CANNOT EXPRESS COLLAPSE AT ALL, ON ANY FIXTURE.** It is not that the spike disagrees about this board — **it has no collapse mechanism to disagree with.** ⇒ **This is D2 and D3 touching: the harness cannot render the shipped Zone C's collapse case even in principle.**
- ⭐ **THE CONSEQUENCE THAT CHANGES THE ESCALATION, AND IT IS GOOD NEWS THAT MUST REACH JONATHAN WITH THE BAD: D2 BITES ONLY *ABOVE* THE CAP.** A fixture of **≤ 8 kinds renders BYTE-IDENTICALLY in both lanes** (both print every row, both print `other_kinds: none`, and the shipped builder's `none` branch is then *correct* rather than merely coincidental). ⇒ ✅ **Every training fixture `tA`–`tF` can be authored at ≤ 8 kinds and is lane-identical BY CONSTRUCTION — the training data is NOT blocked on D2.** ⛔ **Only `t0` is affected — and `t0` is exactly the sealed one, which is why the escalation still stands and still belongs to him.**

### 4. 🔒 THE TWO PRE-REGISTERED STOPS + THE THREE DECISION RULES — **WRITTEN BEFORE M1 RUNS**

- ⛔ **STOP 1 — WIRE FORMAT.** If `chat=0` is **materially below** `chat=1`: **fix the runtime wire format, re-measure, re-ask.** ⛔ **Training is NOT AUTHORISED until the shipped lane has been measured at its own best.**
- ⛔ **STOP 2 — ROSTER SEAM.** If M3's artifact diff fails on the roster block: **resolve D2 first.** Every training prompt would otherwise carry a roster block **the game never emits**, and the sealed holdout would be scoring a board shape **that does not ship.**
- **M2 ≈ M1 within M0's band** ⇒ the gap is **capability**; the mix is class-driven from M5's taxonomy.
- **M2 materially worse than M1** ⇒ the gap is **distribution**; the mix is voice-heavy. Fine-tuning very likely works.
- ⛔ **M2 already ≥ 13/15-equivalent on the shipped shape** ⇒ **DO NOT TRAIN.** Re-run the gate arithmetic — the feature may already pass once the wire format is honest.
- ⚠️ **"MATERIALLY" IS DEFINED BY M0, NOT BY JUDGEMENT.** M0's determinism probe prints the instrument's **noise floor as a number**; a difference inside that band is not a difference. ⛔ **A stop evaluated before M0 has a number has no denominator.**

**⛔⛔ THE STOP-1 FALSE NEGATIVE — FOUND BY TASK-477 (2026-08-03), AND IT IS THE MOST CONSEQUENTIAL DEFECT THIS BATCH HAS TURNED UP. IT WOULD HAVE READ AS GOOD NEWS.**

> ### ⛔ **`BuildPrompt` FALLS BACK TO RAW CONCATENATION *SILENTLY* WHEN THE LOADED GGUF CARRIES NO CHAT TEMPLATE. THE APPLY-*FAILED* PATH WARNS; THE NO-TEMPLATE PATH DOES NOT.**

- ⛔ **⇒ A `chat=1` RUN CAN PRODUCE RAW BYTES.** A secretly **raw-vs-raw** A/B measures **zero difference** and reads as ***"D1 does not exist"*** ⇒ **STOP 1 closes, the batch concludes the wire format is not the problem, and weeks go into QLoRA to fix something a template would have fixed.**
- ⚠️ **NAME THE PROPERTY THAT MAKES THIS CLASS SO DANGEROUS: THE FAILURE IS INVISIBLE *AND IT POINTS THE WRONG WAY*.** A silent failure that produced an alarming number would be caught by the alarm. **This one produces the single most reassuring result available** — the two lanes agree — **and reassurance is not audited.** ⇒ ⚖️ **A NEGATIVE RESULT FROM AN INSTRUMENT WHOSE FAILURE MODE *PRODUCES* NEGATIVE RESULTS IS NOT EVIDENCE.**
- ✅ **THE GUARD, AND IT IS MEASURED OFF THE PRODUCED BYTES RATHER THAN OFF THE REQUEST: `wire=raw|chat` IS PRINTED FROM A POSITIVE STRUCTURAL FACT — `FullPrompt.StartsWith(ZoneA)`** — because **both raw branches put Zone A at offset 0 and a template CANNOT** — **plus a WARNING when `chat=1` came out raw.** ⚖️ **That is a fact about the artifact, not a readback of the flag that was asked for, which is the whole distinction §17 and the relayed-diagnosis law keep re-teaching.**
- ⛔ **BINDING ON M1: THE WIRE READING IS TAKEN ON `SpikePrompt` *FIRST* AND QUOTED BESIDE THE A/B. A `chat=1` run that prints `wire=raw` INVALIDATES THE A/B — it is not a result, it is a broken instrument.**
- ✅ **AND THE PLACEMENT REFUSAL IS RATIFIED: the indicator was deliberately NOT put on `SpikeEval`'s default path**, on the stated ground that ***a new line on the default path moves the instrument every number on record was taken with*** (§16's frozen-default rule). **The refusal was written into `SpikeEval`'s own help text**, where the next operator meets it. ⚖️ **Declining to add a safety print to a frozen path, and documenting why AT the path, is the correct resolution of two rules that genuinely conflict.**

**✅ AND THE QUESTION THE SPEC DEMANDED WAS ANSWERED — IT SHARPENS BOTH STOPS AND IT IS BOARDED AS LAW:**
> ⚖️ **THE ASSEMBLY *RULE* IS BYTE-IDENTICAL; THE RESULTING *BYTES* ARE NOT, AND CANNOT BE.**
- `ComposeTurnPrompt` and `BuildPrompt`'s raw branch **agree on every degree of freedom** — same three parts, **A→B→C order, no separator, no roles, no trailing newline.** ⇒ ⛔ **THE ENTIRE RESIDUAL IS THE ZONE BUILDERS** (the 3029-vs-5116 Zone-A divergence, and D2's roster collapse) — **which is M3's territory, not this flag's.**
- ⇒ ✅ **`chat=` CONTROLS THE WIRE FORMAT AND ONLY THAT.** ⚖️ **THEREFORE STOP 1 AND STOP 2 TEST GENUINELY DIFFERENT THINGS AND NEITHER CAN ABSORB THE OTHER'S FAILURE** — a separation that has to be *established*, not assumed, because two stops that overlap can both pass while the union fails.
- ⚠️ **D1's FIX CARRIES ITS OWN TRAP AND IT IS SILENT: `SetStaticPrefix` MUST REGISTER THE *TEMPLATED* PREFIX** (system-open + Zone A + system-close + user-open), **not bare Zone A.** Register the wrong one and **KV reuse dies at token ~3, silently degrading bars #2 and #3 with NO LOG LINE.** ⇒ Lock the **pre-template half** with a frozen-transcription test (`SiegeAssistantPromptWireTest.cpp`, copying `SiegeAssistantZoneATest.cpp`'s idiom); ⛔ **the wrapper half lives inside the GGUF and can only be ARTIFACT-CHECKED — say so plainly rather than over-claiming a test.**
